### Go to Fyxestroll Garden with Guinaifen

> *You see Guinaifen, and she's as beautiful in person as she is online... Ahem, we've digressed. The girl is trying very hard to convince you that she just experienced a spooky encounter, even shoving a video of it in your face. Even video content can't be trusted these days, but her flustered tone and unbridled expressions do hint at some kind of terror surrounding that Fyxestroll Garden place... Heh, the Nameless shall uncover the secret behind all this! Let's go ghostbusting at Fyxestroll Garden! Right now, you won't get it — but curiosity forms the basis for the start of all good horror movies...*

*(Upon arrival in Fyxestroll Garden)*

**Guinaifen:** It's so dark in here... It feels like we're being watched...

> **桂乃芬：**唔，四周黑黢黢的，总感觉好像有人在暗处盯着咱们……

**Guinaifen:** Did anyone ever tell you the scary stories about Fyxestroll Garden?

> **桂乃芬：**不知道家人之前听说过这绥园闹鬼的传闻吗？

- ▸ Don't think so...

**Guinaifen:** Then allow me! As a streamer, I've done my fair share of research. Let me find my script...

> **桂乃芬：**问得好！作为探景主播我可是做足了功课。等我找一下视频文案……

**Guinaifen:** "Wandering Fox Spirits and Ghostly Tails..." Fyxestroll Garden was originally a garden delve constructed by a Foxian craftmaster. It was a place for their kin to rest, write poems, and feast... et cetera et cetera.

> **桂乃芬：**「绥绥仙狐，颀尾庞庞」，绥园本是一位狐人巧匠打造的园林洞天，供同胞们休憩闲游、赋诗宴饮的地方…以下五百字省略。

**Guinaifen:** It's said the elite pilots of the Foxians were almost wiped out in the Denizens of Abundance Wars thirty years ago. People went to the Foxsomn Tomb in Fyxestroll Garden to grieve for the departed... Because of this, Fyxestroll Garden rarely saw visitors and was bereft of joy.

> **桂乃芬：**据说在三十年前丰饶民战争中，狐族飞行士精锐尽出，死伤甚众。人们自发到绥园里的狐眠冢处吊唁故人…这让绥园游人稀疏，少有欢声。

**Guinaifen:** Over time, Fyxestroll Garden became full of flickering ghostly flames, and weeping lamentations echoed through the air. The rumor that the garden was haunted spread like wildfire among Xianzhou Luofu residents.

> **桂乃芬：**久而久之，绥园幽幽鬼火忽明忽暗，悲泣幽咽之声凭空回荡，「绥园闹鬼」的消息在罗浮仙舟居民间不胫而走。

- ▸ Sounds like the hauntings in Fyxestroll Garden could be true after all?

**Guinaifen:** I know, right? I did a lot of research before I decided to film here...

> **桂乃芬：**没错，我来绥园取材，有过充分的调查。

- ▸ ...Aaaand who wrote that for you?

**Guinaifen:** Ugh, man, is it that obvious? I paid Mr. Xiyan to write it up for me. Sounded a little too professional, huh?

> **桂乃芬：**家人啊，看破不说破。这条视频文案是花钱请西衍先生帮我写的，能不专业吗？

- ▸ Could you make it quick?

**Guinaifen:** Sure, I guess now's not really a good time for stories... Don't worry — I can sum it up in a single sentence.

> **桂乃芬：**嗯，眼下确实也不适合长篇大论。放心，我用一句话将来龙去脉给你整明白。

**Guinaifen:** Fyxestroll Garden used to be a garden delve, but people started coming here to mourn fallen Foxian fighters at the Foxsomn Tomb, and rumors spread that the garden was haunted.

> **桂乃芬：**这绥园是一处园林洞天，不过后来有人在狐眠冢吊唁阵亡的狐族将士，这里便渐渐传出了「绥园闹鬼」的传闻。

**Guinaifen:** I know it's easy to cast doubt, but the Ten-Lords Commission actually showed up this time.

> **桂乃芬：**闹鬼传闻虽然听起来荒诞，但这次十王司来人可是真的。

**Guinaifen:** Speaking of which, we have to avoid running into the judges. If we get kicked out, it'll all be for nothing.

> **桂乃芬：**说到这个，要是前面有什么风吹草动，咱俩可千万要绕开走。不说撞见鬼，万一遇见了判官，咱俩肯定会被赶出来，这一趟就算是白来了。

**Guinaifen:** Anyway, that's the background. Let's keep moving, and watch your step...

> **桂乃芬：**好了，该嘱咐的都嘱咐完了，咱接着走，当心脚下。

### Explore the Fyxestroll Garden! Bring it on, schmucks! Huh!

> *You two — yes, only you two — arrive at the long-abandoned Fyxestroll Garden. The protagonists in most horror movies always like to brashly break into derelict buildings that are rumored to be haunted, completely disregarding all safety concerns and bringing in only a small party. If only they stopped to think things over, then none of the stories that follow would ever have happened. On the way over, Guinaifen diligently recounts for you how the Fyxestroll Garden ended up getting abandoned... all the while shooting plenty of spooky footage along the way. You can't help but feel it's actually quite hard for internet influencers to make a living, especially if they have to keep taking photos while resisting the urge to run away from such an ominous place, like a reporter at a homicide scene. This place really does feel as though it's haunted... Where, then, are the ghosts?*

**Guinaifen:** Whoa, this place feels pure evil... Hee-hee, time to get some footage.

**Guinaifen:** I wonder if this thumbnail would be approved...

**Guinaifen:** Shh, follow me. Keep quiet and tread lightly...

> **桂乃芬：**嗯，这里气氛骇人。我正好录一些素材，嘿嘿。

*(Attempt to approach Ten-Lords Commission spiritfarers)*

**Guinaifen:** Wait, wait, wait — stop.

> **桂乃芬：**嘶…这张照片用做视频封面，能过审吗？

**Guinaifen:** ...There are Ten-Lords Commission spiritfarers on patrol. Let's go the other way — we'll head to the Foxsomn Tomb first, then cut through the Locufox Forest.

**Hanya:** At ease, Wraith Warden. These two are not your enemies.

**Aurumaton Spectral Envoy:** Understood, Your Honor.

> **金人勾魂使：**是，判官大人。

**Hanya:** As a member of the Ten-Lords Commission, you may as well consider me a member of the dead. I am a judge, alias "Hanya" — responsible for the four duties of detention, incarceration, interrogation, and punishment. Therefore, I know a thing or two about you.

> **寒鸦：**入得十王司，你们尽可以把我当成已死之人了。小女子代号「寒鸦」，身为判官，执掌拘、锁、刑、问四部职责之末。故而，我对你们二位并非一无所知。

**Hanya:** Guinaifen, an Outworlder, unemployed... You entered Fyxestroll Garden with Sushang, a Cloud Knight, the day before yesterday.

> **寒鸦：**桂乃芬，化外民，无业…于前日和云骑士卒素裳进入绥园。

**Guinaifen:** Huh? Unemployed? I'm a street performer and a streamer who reports on current events! A streamer! It's a legit operation!

> **桂乃芬：**欸，无业？是街头行为艺术家和寻找时事热点的主播啦！主播！好歹是个正经的营生！

**Hanya:** As for this one here...

> **寒鸦：**至于这边这个……

- ▸ I'm (Trailblazer). Nice to meet you.

**Xueyi:** ...A passenger on the Astral Express and a guest of the Seat of Divine Foresight. You assisted the Cloud Knights in resolving the Ambrosial Arbor calamity and are a friend of the Alliance.

> **雪衣：**…星穹列车乘员，神策府的客人，帮助云骑平息了建木灾厄，是联盟的朋友。

**Huohuo:** H-Hello... I'm a Judge of the Ten-Lords Commission. My name is Huohuo.

> **藿藿：**你、你好…我是十王司判官，我叫藿藿。

**Xueyi:** As a Judge of the Ten-Lords Commission, you should speak with more confidence, Huohuo.

> **雪衣：**藿藿，你已是十王司判官了，说话要再有底气一些。

**Huohuo:** ...O—Okay.

> **藿藿：**…是、是。

- ▸ Your Honor, it was all Guinaifen's idea!

**Guinaifen:** A—Are you really selling me out that fast? Whatever, I'll take the hit... It's true, I persuaded to come along.

> **桂乃芬：**这、这么快就把我出卖了吗？好吧，一人做事一人当，确实是我劝他/她一起来的。

**Hanya:** Regardless whose idea it was... This place has been designated as a restricted area. No one is allowed to enter.

> **寒鸦：**不管是谁的主意…两位，这儿已被划为禁区，无干人等不得入内。

- ▸ You must have heard of... the Galactic Baseballer.

**Xueyi:** You are a passenger on the Astral Express and a guest of the Seat of Divine Foresight. You assisted the Cloud Knights in resolving the Ambrosial Arbor calamity and are a friend of the Alliance.

> **雪衣：**开拓者，星穹列车乘员，神策府的客人，帮助云骑平息了建木灾厄，是联盟的朋友。

**Huohuo:** ... the Nameless? From the train?

> **藿藿：**…他/她就是星穹列车上的无名客？

**Hanya:** Tell me, Guinaifen. Why did you return here?

> **寒鸦：**说说吧，桂乃芬，去而复返，所为何事？

**Guinaifen:** Uh, no reason. I haven't posted anything for ages, so I wanted to film some ghosts for my videos... hehe.

> **桂乃芬：**呃，也没什么，就是好久没有更新了，想拍些有关幽灵鬼怪的影像，当作视频素材…嘿嘿。

**Xueyi:** We have already told you there are no ghosts on the Xianzhou — it is a ridiculous superstition. As a resident of the Xianzhou, you should refrain from believing or spreading rumors.

> **雪衣：**吾已说过，仙舟之上并无鬼魂。这是荒诞迷信，身为仙舟住民，理当不信谣不传谣。

**Guinaifen:** But I saw them! They had ghostly flames coming out of their body, and they were cackling! And Sushang saw them too—

> **桂乃芬：**但我明明看见了，那些家伙冒着吱吱鬼火，发出桀桀怪笑，再说了，素裳也瞧见了——

**Huohuo:** Miss Guinaifen, it's hard to explain, but those things aren't ghosts. Please don't bring legends from your home world onto the Xianzhou...

> **藿藿：**桂乃芬小姐，虽然一时半会儿很难解释清楚，但那些东西并不是你以为的幽灵鬼怪，请不要把家乡的传说套在仙舟的事情上……

**Huohuo:** The roaming entities you saw are extremely dangerous energy parasites... We call them "heliobi."

> **藿藿：**那些溜出来作祟的东西，是非常危险的能量寄生物「岁阳」。

**Guinaifen:** ...I... Really? I don't believe it. "Heliobi"? What's that? Ohh, I get it — "heliobi" is just another word for "ghost" on the Xianzhou, right? That's gotta be it.

> **桂乃芬：**真的吗？我不信。岁阳？那是什么？我明白啦，在仙舟上，岁阳就是幽灵的别称，对吧？一定是这么回事。

**Huohuo:** Maybe we should let Mr. Tail out for a bit... M—Mr. Tail?

> **藿藿：**看来还是得让尾巴大爷出来一趟…尾、尾巴大爷？

**???:** I heard you, I heard you! Tsk, unbelievable... What am I, her pet?

> **？？？：**听到了听到了！吵死了…本大爷又不是伺候人类的珍禽异兽。

**Guinaifen:** Mr. Tail...?

> **桂乃芬：**尾巴…大爷？

**Tail:** I'm a heliobus! We're not ghosts. Got it? Now scram!

> **尾巴：**老子就是岁阳，就是你以为的幽灵鬼怪，看明白了吗？看明白了就滚！

**Guinaifen:** ...

> **桂乃芬：**……

**Guinaifen:** GHOOOOOOOOST!!!

> **桂乃芬：**鬼啊！

**Hanya:** What a strange girl. Here to film ghosts, yet terrified when she lays eyes on one...

> **寒鸦：**哎呀，真是个奇怪的小姑娘，明明就是为了拍摄灵异鬼怪而来的嘛，怎么真见到了反而大呼小叫起来了……

**Guinaifen:** Deep breaths, Little Gui, deep breaths...! Ahem, c—can I have a small chat with this... "Mr. Tail?"

> **桂乃芬：**深呼吸！小桂子，冷静！深呼吸！欸，能不能让我和这、这个尾巴大爷再聊两句？

**Tail:** I said scram. I have no time to talk nonsense with you.

> **尾巴：**滚，老子没空跟你吹牛放屁。

- ▸ What a rude ghost!

> **尾巴：**说了好几遍老子不是幽灵，再啰嗦就把你们都吃了！

**Huohuo:** Please don't be afraid, he's only kidding. Mr. Tail is a vegetarian.

> **藿藿：**两位别怕，它只是说说的，尾巴大爷它吃素呢。

**Tail:** Finally, someone with common sense.

> **尾巴：**这小子/小姑娘挺有眼力见。

**Guinaifen:** N—No way! This is a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity to get something for my vids!

> **桂乃芬：**不能撤，这可是个千载难逢的取材机会！

**Guinaifen:** Your Honor, if I may ask, what exactly is this... fire thing? How can we prevent it from hurting people? ...And why is a judge carrying one around with her...?

> **桂乃芬：**判官大人，冒昧问下，这火焰团子到底是个什么东西？该如何防范它暴起伤人？还有还有…为什么判官大人会随身带着这么个东西……

**Xueyi:** I believe we're done here. We have important matters to attend to. Huohuo, please see them off.

> **雪衣：**该见的都见了，判官还有要事处理。藿藿，送客。

**Huohuo:** Y—Yes, ma'am.

> **藿藿：**遵、遵命。

**Hanya:** Sister, don't be angry. Most of your time is spent in the field — you're not accustomed to dealing with others. Leave this to me. If I explain the reasoning, they will leave.

> **寒鸦：**姐姐别生气，您平时外出执行任务，少有待人接物的经验，这件事不妨交给寒鸦处置，只要我能说明原因，他们自会离开。

**Hanya:** As things stand, the situation in Fyxestroll Garden is sure to keep drawing attention. If we forcibly dismiss these two today, their curiosity will remain — who knows what might happen if they decide to come back. They might not be lucky enough to run into us next time.

> **寒鸦：**眼下绥园的异状不可避免地会引来关注，防不胜防。今天粗暴打发了不明就里的二人，他们心有不甘，万一再来，却不一定能有再遇上我们的好运。

**Hanya:** As I said, sister, I will handle this.

> **寒鸦：**放心交给我吧，姐姐。

**Hanya:** I assume the two of you heard about the damage done to the Artisanship Commission's Creation Furnace? The "heliobi" sealed within escaped and have been haunting this place. The Ten-Lords Commission has orders to capture them. I am strongly advising you to leave — for your own safety.

> **寒鸦：**二位想必听说过工造司「造化洪炉」破损一事。其中封锁的「岁阳」妖物走脱，盘踞此处，十王司正受命抓捕。劝你们速速离开，也是为了安全着想。

**Hanya:** If you have any questions, raise them now — I will do my best to satisfy your curiosity. But I hope you will keep any information you hear in strict confidence... and then leave Fyxestroll Garden — never to return.

**Guinaifen:** Th—That's very reasonable, Your Honor! Thank you. I promise we'll leave after asking our questions.

> **桂乃芬：**这位判官大人真是通情达理，谢谢啦。我发誓，问完就走。

**Hanya:** As a clerk at the Hall of Karma, my knowledge on these matters is somewhat limited, but I will try my best to satisfy your curiosity. Please proceed.

> **寒鸦：**寒鸦身为因果殿录事者，虽然谈不上无所不知，但会尽量满足你们的好奇心，问吧。

- ▸ What exactly are heliobi?

**Hanya:** According to the Divination Commission's classification, they are energy parasites from the cosmos itself. They are "Starfire Essence" that materialize in the form of strange fire.

> **寒鸦：**若按太卜司的分类，它们是来自天外的能量寄生物，以妖异火焰形象出现的「星火之精」。

**Hanya:** Metaphorically speaking, they are like moths to a flame... but that flame is the thoughts of sentient beings.

> **寒鸦：**若用比喻来说，它们就像喜爱追逐热源的飞蛾。只不过这「热源」不是别的，而是有情生灵的思想。

- ▸ Are heliobi dangerous?

**Hanya:** Yes. They attach themselves to sentient beings, feeding off their host's experiences and emotions.

> **寒鸦：**嗯。它们喜爱寄生在有智慧的生命身上，汲取他们的体验，品尝他们的情绪。

**Hanya:** A heliobus will slowly take root in its host's nervous system, using their deepest desires and weaknesses to create wild delusions. They manipulate their host like a chess piece. The host has no choice but to obey the heliobus, so we refer to it as "possession."

> **寒鸦：**渐渐地，岁阳会扎根宿主的神经系统，利用其内心的欲望和弱点，让他产生种种颠倒妄想，如同操纵棋子傀儡般摆弄宿主。宿主则会对岁阳言听计从，我们称之为「夺舍」。

**Hanya:** By that point, the host is no longer who they once were, but a heliobus both similar to and completely different from their past self. The host's body will soon be exhausted by the heliobus — like burnt firewood.

> **寒鸦：**到了这一步，被寄生者脑袋里装的已经不再是过去的自己了，而是一个与自己相似却截然不同的岁阳。宿主的身躯很快会被岁阳耗干殆尽，就像被焚烧燃尽的柴薪。

- ▸ So, Tail is dangerous, too?

**Tail:** Dangerous!? You don't know the half of it! The other heliobi are nothing compared to me!

> **尾巴：**臭小子/小姑娘怎么说话的！什么叫「也很危险」？老子就是最危险的那一挂！

**Huohuo:** Tail... was subdued and sealed in my tail by Madam Hanya. He won't harm others.

> **藿藿：**尾巴…已被寒鸦大人镇伏在我尾巴上了，不会危害别人的。

**Hanya:** Regrettably, heliobi are not flesh-and-blood, and so they are difficult to destroy. Before the restoration of the Creation Furnace, the Ten-Lords Commission plans to set up an "Evil Binding Matrix" in this abandoned garden to imprison these abominations.

> **寒鸦：**令人头疼的是，岁阳并非血肉凡物，难以灭去。所以在修复「造化洪炉」前，十王司打算在这座废园里立起「束形却邪阵」，囚缚这些妖物。

- ▸ I don't have any other questions.

**Hanya:** As you can see, the Evil Binding Matrix has not yet been completed, and not all the escaped heliobi have been recovered. This area is still very dangerous. I will ask Judge Huohuo to escort you out of the garden.

**Guinaifen:** "Not all the escaped heliobi have been recovered..." So that means the other supernatural stuff that's been happening on the Luofu might be related? Your Honor! I have loads of info sources! Maybe I can help you!

**Xueyi:** Unnecessary. Those not involved should leave now. Go.

> **雪衣：**不需要！无关人等即刻离开，送客。

**Huohuo:** Um... Excuse me... We should go.

> **藿藿：**那个…两位…我们该走了。

### Chat with Huohuo, and get her to guide you out

> *You don't see a ghost, but rather something even more horrifying: a Judge of the Ten-Lords Commission! Despite reason telling you these three Judges are all human beings, each one of them seems more terrifying than the next: the emaciated little girl housing a spirit within her, the burly giantess with the white curtain bangs and faint voice, and the only one you've met before, Xueyi, is a mechanical puppet. A ghost is nothing compared to that. The Judges patiently explain it in layman's terms to convince you and Guinaifen to return: There are no ghosts aboard the Xianzhou, just some extraterrestrial parasites that have escaped millennia of imprisonment. Don't wander around here for no reason, as it hinders us from carrying out our official duties. Good news — There are no ghosts running around Fyxestroll Garden! Bad news — There's an alien parasite running around Fyxestroll Garden... Isn't that even scarier!*

> **桂乃芬：**…不过要是路上能拍下几段关于尾巴大爷的视频，起个「绥园惊现灵异鬼火，附身无辜狐人女孩」的标题，应该还挺抓眼球的？

**Guinaifen:** ...Can you give me a hand?

### Find Guinaifen in Locufox Forest

> *Huohuo escorted you both all the way out of the Fyxestroll Garden. However, a sudden eerie wind blew, and thick fog enveloped the surroundings. When you looked back, Guinaifen had vanished... ### Find the exit of the Illusory Maze

> *You often see similar news stories about a person getting lost while trying to find another missing person. Unfortunately, today's the day you are destined to be the protagonist of such a story. Gradually, you manage to lose track of even Huohuo. After sizing up a strange bronze mirror for a moment, you are somehow pulled into a realm of illusion. There's walls and doors everywhere, sending you round in circles and disorienting you. According to some of the Xianzhou folk tales you've heard, the outside world will have passed hundreds of years by the time you leave here — you don't want to spend the rest of your life lost in a maze, so find the exit pronto!*

### Search out clues from supernatural stories

> *Through Cirrus' guidance, you discover a ripped page from a bizarre story recounting events surrounding the heliobi and the Luofu's general from centuries ago. Its writing style seems to hint at a way to see through the illusory maze.*

*(Approach the marked location)*

> **浮烟：**嘿，我让「你眼中的纸人」飘起来了，你也多注意一下呀。

*(Upon walking away from the crates)*

**Cirrus:** Hey, hey! I'm trying to help you. Go and look at the clues.

> **浮烟：**喂喂喂，难得别人好心为你，你还是看看那边的线索吧。

**Cirrus:** ...Do I have to make you drag yourself over there?

> **浮烟：**…我可不想按着你的手让你来看。

*(Investigate crates)*

*(Quick Read)*

Nighttales of Fyxestroll Garden: Scene One (Part 2)

... It is said that, in his mansion that day, General Teng Xiao was in deep thought, devising strategies and conducting simulations to break the encirclement of the borisins' forces. Suddenly, a strange wind blew through the hall, causing the curtains to flutter. General Teng Xiao stood up with his hands behind his back and spoke to the empty room. "It is impolite to be an uninvited guest, and even more so to hide in the shadows. Show yourself quickly!" Laughter echoed through the empty hall and suddenly, a formless green flame appeared, resembling a ghostly eye, fixating its eerie gaze on the general. "I've heard that each of the Reignbow's Arbiter-Generals possess exceptional abilities, and seeing you today confirm those rumors." "I entered these halls without prior notice, and for that, I apologize. However, my purpose here is to assist you in breaking the encirclement." "The borisins' forces are closing in with the intention of completely annihilating the Luofu. Your troops are few, and resisting the enemy is as difficult as reaching the heavens." Although the green flame had no mouth, it seemed to converse like a person. It hovered through the air, illuminating the dark corners of the hall. The guards in the hall stood expressionlessly, as if trapped in a dream. "Facing life and death in battle, mortals tremble in fear. But if you allow us, the heliobi, to possess you, you'll have no fear of the blade or the enemy. Defeating them will be as easy as turning your hand." "Your help freely offered must come with an exchange. I've heard that the Reignbow once exchanged their mortal body for aid from the Flint Emperor. I assume you have similar intentions?" "I, Ignamar, seek nothing more than your body and emotions, General of the Reignbow." The jade flame burned brighter, growing to a size hundredfold bigger than before. "I have been dormant for a millennium, and there's no better corporeal offering for us than this!" The general remained unfazed, signaling Ignamar to continue. "At midnight tonight, I shall await your answer in Fyxestroll Garden. At the garden entrance, there will be several porcelain vases, some cerulean and some vermillion. If you refuse my offer, leave the cerulean vases pristine and unharmed. At the same time, with the vermilion vases, you should break them all if you accept my offer and wish for bloody battle." "That is how I will be able to decipher your reply, general. After tonight, if you wish to meet me again, knock with the left door knocker three times, followed by the right knocker three times. No more, no less." The general frowned and sighed, "A mere malevolent spirit dares to steal one's soul and mimic human behavior. Away, and I will respond in time." ...

**Cirrus:** Do you understand? This story of the supernatural is in the illusory maze for a reason. It contains the clue to help us get out of here. Come back and read it again if you need to.

> **浮烟：**你看明白了吗？这篇志怪话本出现在幻障迷境里是有原因的，话本里藏着离开这里的提示，你要是记不住，就再来看一遍。

*(Quick Read)*

Nighttales of Fyxestroll Garden: Scene One (Part 3)

... Speaking of Fyxestroll Garden, there's a place within its boundaries known as Locufox Forest, which was built by Foxian craftsmen for the enjoyment of the garden's visitors. Over the years, they expanded it with elegant buildings, marking it with exceptional opulence. General Teng Xiao entered this area, but instead of seeing its beauty, he found it in ruins and desolation, filled with eerie and sinister atmospheres. He wondered how a place like this could fall into such disrepair when Fyxestroll Garden had only been completed decades prior. He suspected this was the work of heliobi's illusions. He arrived at a courtyard and was greeted by a young servant who bowed and offered him a food box. When he examined it closely, he noticed that the young servant had milky eyes, indicating impairment of sight. "The food is simple, as a mere token of goodwill. You've come a long way, hungry and tired, so my master wishes for you to dine before we discuss matters further." "I've passed through at least ten moon gates to get here, but in all my visits to Fyxestroll Garden, I don't remember encountering so many gates. If your master wishes to negotiate, they should do so openly and with respect. Where does it find the audacity to play tricks on me?" The young servant remained unperturbed by Teng Xiao's stern words, as if deaf to his thunderous reprimand. "With the tricks your master has played with dark arts, if I were to eat from this box, I'd probably find a lump of black mud in my mouth." With a flick of his sleeves, General Teng Xiao tossed the food box onto the ground. Sure enough, it grew spider-like limbs and skittered away. "General, with your divine insight and clear vision, you've already passed the test. As a token of acknowledgment, my master wishes to present you with a gift," answered the blind servant calmly. "In the side room, there are various chests to choose from. Please select the least expensive one." "As for the porcelain vases at the entrance..." The blind young servant's face twisted into a disturbing smile, no longer resembling that of an innocent child. "My master said: 'General, after enduring so much on the battlefield, I'm sure you'd show compassion for the wounded and fallen soldiers. Please break all the cerulean vases. As for the vermilion ones, they must remain intact.'" "It seems that we are guaranteed to strike this deal." The general let out a long sigh. He looked up and found that the clear moon had disappeared, replaced by thick, enveloping clouds. "Let's hope your master doesn't come to regret its decision." Teng Xiao strode forward, pushing open the final gate. ...

### Chat with Xueyi and learn the truth about Fyxestroll Garden

> *Being dragged into a mirror and then spat out again makes a perfect beginning and end for a supernatural story. Unfortunately, as soon as you leave the illusory maze, you realize that the real Fyxestroll Garden has fallen under the control of the heliobi, and the Judges are doing their best to prolong the impending utter rout.*

**Huohuo:** Are we... back in Fyxestroll Garden?

> **藿藿：**我们…似乎回到了绥园里？

**Xueyi:** You have returned safely. It seems the Ten-Lords Commission is not down on our luck today.

**Huohuo:** Madam Xueyi, I'm so happy to see you're alright!

> **藿藿：**雪衣大人，你没事真是太好了！

> **浮烟：**嘻嘻，真不愧是判官啊，警惕的很哪。

**Huohuo informs Xueyi of what you have experienced so far...:**

**Xueyi:** I understand. This isn't the time to deal with Cirrus. Fyxestroll Garden has been dragged into a powerful illusion by an even mightier heliobus, and the spiritfarers who went to suppress it have lost contact with me.

> **雪衣：**情况吾已明了，不过眼下暂时不是操心浮烟的时候。绥园已被某个更强大的岁阳拖入了幻境，前去镇伏的冥差们也都已失去音信。

**Xueyi:** Huohuo, if both Hanya and I are unable to suppress that demon, then you will have to defeat it.

> **雪衣：**藿藿，若吾与寒鸦上阵依然无法制住那妖物，对付它的重担就落到汝身上了。

**Huohuo:** Huh? I-I won't be able to do it! I'm not as powerful as you two... I always run and hide before a battle...

> **藿藿：**哎？我…我不行的。我不像两位大人那样有本事…我只会在战斗时躲在一边瑟瑟发抖。

**Xueyi:** Do not underestimate yourself. A heliobus cannot possess a body that is already possessed by another heliobus — such is your absolute advantage as a judge.

> **雪衣：**不要妄自菲薄。岁阳无法占夺由另一只岁阳依附的肉身——这便是汝身为判官的绝对优势。

- ▸ I'm possessed too — do I also get an advantage?

**Xueyi:** Correct. However, Huohuo has had many more years of training and is more experienced in fighting heliobi than you.

> **雪衣：**说的不错。不过藿藿饱经训练，论对抗岁阳更有心得。

**Xueyi:** That is why the Ten-Lords Commission is not down on our luck today. We're lucky that you have all returned at this opportune time.

> **雪衣：**所以，汝等能在此时平安归来，十王司的运气不算太糟。

**Tail:** You hear that, scaredy-cat? You're the ace of the Ten-Lords Commission.

> **尾巴：**听懂了吗，怂包？打醒精神，眼前这队十王司的人马里，没一个能强过你！

- ▸ Indeed. Even the weakest person has their strengths.

**Huohuo:** I know you're trying to comfort me... but that sounded a little weird.

> **藿藿：**虽然知道开拓者先生/小姐是想安慰我…但，这话听着高兴不起来啊。

> **尾巴：**啰嗦，挑后半段爱听的听就好了！

**Xueyi:** The heliobi are aggressive beings, but they cannot shake their desire for a flesh and blood body. And yet, during the 38 years you have worked for the Ten Lords, you have been constantly able to suppress the monster within your tail and maintain your personality. You are already a fitting judge. Be confident.

> **雪衣：**岁阳虽然凶悍，却始终不能摆脱对血肉凡身的渴望。但汝却能时刻降服妖物保持常性，为十王效力三十八载。汝已是一名合格的判官，应当再自信一些。

> **尾巴：**呸呸呸，老子才不和口粮交朋友！

### Go to Swallowsong Pavilion as proposed by Cirrus and chat with Furynox

> *Cirrus claims to know Furynox like the back of its hand, and proposes a game changer of a counter. But does this shifty creature really want to help humanity win? Or could it be that it secretly harbors ulterior motives? We'll never know what's up its sleeve unless we let it play its trump card. You're nervous, but you have to hold on until that trump card gets played.*

**Cirrus:** You've heard the story of Ignamar and the great heliobus's glorious fight against the general. They were an entity born of many heliobi fusing together...

> **浮烟：**各位都知道大岁阳「燎原」激战将军的故事，它是众多岁阳融聚一体的结果。

**Cirrus:** ...but heliobi are gradually tainted by the emotions and desires of those we possess. We tend to spite each other and don't like to fuse together — it's like having multiple personalities inside a single head. It leads to "infighting..."

> **浮烟：**不过，咱们岁阳各自沾染着附身宿主时获得的情绪和欲望，彼此互相嫌弃，没有谁愿意和谁融在一起——就像一个人脑袋里有「多重人格」，时不时得打架。

**Cirrus:** A powerful heliobus like Ignamar or Furynox might be able to swallow a lesser heliobus and placate them for a while...

> **浮烟：**强大的岁阳也许能吞下弱小的岁阳，让它们暂时屈从成为一体——比如「燎原」，或者咱们要对付的这个「恚炎」。

**Cirrus:** ...but this internal balance is easily broken when exposed to external forces — just like how we were torn apart and reborn after Ignamar fought the general.

> **浮烟：**不过一遇外力刺激，这种平衡也很容易崩溃。这不，「燎原」就在与将军大战后成了咱们。

**Xueyi:** Therefore... this might be the way to defeat Furynox. We can cause them to break apart and reduce their power, then the Ten-Lords Commission can use their sacred vessels to seal up all of Furynox's fragments.

> **雪衣：**所以，诱使恚炎崩溃…这个方法也许可行。趁着恚炎分裂，力量削弱，十王司可以用法器将它的碎片挨个封印起来。

**Xueyi:** I have a question. Why are you betraying your own kin? Why would you help the Xianzhou when we have sealed you for so long?

> **雪衣：**但是，汝为什么要背叛同族？为什么要帮助镇伏汝等的仙舟？

**Cirrus:** Why? Must heliobi always have a reason for their behavior? Perhaps I don't want Furynox to devour me. Perhaps I dislike idiots who use force and nothing else. Perhaps I wish to fight the general myself...

> **浮烟：**理由？岁阳行事一定需要什么理由吗？也许是因为我不想让恚炎吞掉自己，也许是我瞧不上这个只会使用蛮力的傻大个，也许是我想和将军一斗……

**Cirrus:** ...or maybe I just want to do a favor for the Ten-Lords Commission that will ensure I can leave here in one piece?

> **浮烟：**又也许，我想和十王司交换一个「放我离开」的承诺？

- ▸ We should allow Cirrus to go ahead with their plan.
- ▸ Don't believe Cirrus!

**Xueyi:** Judges of the Ten-Lords Commission always keep our promises. If you can truly deal with the problem at hand, I will ask for your freedom from the Ten Lords. However, I cannot guarantee the outcome of the appeal.

> **雪衣：**十王司的判官绝不空许承诺。若能化解眼前危机，吾可以上禀十王，还汝自由。但结果，吾不保证。

**Cirrus:** How conventional. Fine, I accept... but you better keep your promise, puppet.

> **浮烟：**哎哟，真是个死脑筋。那么今天就只好吃亏一点，做一趟货到付款的买卖了。偃偶，要守信喔。

**Cirrus:** Hehe, me and Furynox were "furnacemates..." I know them well. They are powerful, but as naive as a child and obsessed with victory. I only need to say three things to incite them.

> **浮烟：**呵呵，我与恚炎同为关在洪炉中的狱友，彼此知根知底。它力量虽强，但心思却比孩子还幼稚，一味执着于「胜负」。我只需要三句话，管教它不攻自破。

**Cirrus:** (Trailblazer), I'll need your body for a while longer.

> **浮烟：**开拓者，我要继续借你的身体一用。

*(Approach the marked location)*

**(Trailblazer):** Furynox! Furynox...!

> **开拓者：**恚炎！恚炎——！

*There is a vague sound from far away. A response, possibly.*

**(Trailblazer):** Before you duel the general, why don't you fight me?

> **开拓者：**与将军的决斗还早，你我先一决胜负如何？

**Distant Voice:** ...Cirrus? What makes you worth my time, weakling?

> **遥远的声音：**你是…浮烟？小玩意，你凭什么与我争胜？

**Distant Voice:** I've turned this garden into my territory and made the dogs of the Ten Lords my puppets! As soon as the general arrives, the heliobi will have our rematch. Why should I fight my own at this crucial moment?

> **遥远的声音：**我已将园林化为阵地，教十王司的走狗成了傀儡！只等将军一来，便可重开当年那一战。为什么要在此时和同族对垒？

**(Trailblazer):** That group of amateurs you've put together? Hah. They're nothing compared to me.

> **开拓者：**你操使那些乌合之众又算什么能耐，不过是些臭鱼烂虾，不堪一击。

**(Trailblazer):** I have already obtained the most wonderful pawn for myself — a piece that once expelled Phantylia!

> **开拓者：**我却不同，机缘巧合下我已夺舍了一枚绝好的棋子——一枚曾驱走过幻胧的绝好棋子！

**Distant Voice:** ...Phantylia? Phantylia! I remember! What a wonderful host... I want it!

> **遥远的声音：**…幻胧？…幻胧！我记起来啦！我记起来啦！这么好的棋子，我也想要！

**(Trailblazer):** Then send the spiritfarers under your control to find me. If you can defeat this Nameless I'm possessing, then you can swallow me up and let my strength aid you in your fight against the general. What do you say?

> **开拓者：**你尽可以差遣手中的冥差来找我，若能击败我所占据的这位无名客，我就心甘情愿被你吞下，为你大战将军贡献一臂之力。你意下如何？

**Distant Voice:** Deal! My pawns and I will show you no mercy!

> **遥远的声音：**一言为定！我和我的棋子们，这就来找你！

**Cirrus:** See? Easy-peasy.

> **浮烟：**瞧瞧，轻松搞定。

**Tail:** Tsk, does that mean all the spiritfarers out there have fallen? What a useless army.

> **尾巴：**啧啧，按恚炎的说法，外面的冥差们岂不是全军覆没了？真没用！

**Huohuo:** ...Does that mean we'll be fighting our friends...?

**Xueyi:** ...

> **雪衣：**……

**Cirrus:** Make the decision. Furynox's pawns — your companions — must be destroyed.

**Xueyi:** They've consumed too many heliobi...

> **雪衣：**那家伙聚合了太多岁阳…

**Xueyi:** We'd die from exhaustion before we manage to weaken it.

> **雪衣：**要想一点点削弱它，吾等累死了也没有胜算。

**Tail:** We've still got a chance, puppet...

> **尾巴：**未必没有胜算啊，人偶…

**Tail:** Cirrus said it themselves. There's a fragile balance when heliobi fuse together.

> **尾巴：**按它自己的话说，岁阳融聚时会形成脆弱的平衡。

**Tail:** You know what to do, don't you, Huohuo?

> **尾巴：**你明白该怎么做吧，藿藿？

> **藿藿：**可…可是……

> **尾巴：**怕什么？老子没了，你不就能过上你想要的生活了吗？

**Huohuo:** ...

> **藿藿：**……

**Cirrus:** Ugh, these pawns are no fun. Why don't you join in too, hmm?

> **浮烟：**倦了倦了，这些小棋子太无趣了，不如你们也加入到其中来，如何？

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Cirrus:** Why struggle? Be my subject!

> **浮烟：**放弃抵抗，为我所用！

**Huohuo:** We can't beat them...

> **藿藿：**咱们输定啦……

**Tail:** Not so fast... you've got me, haven't you!?

> **尾巴：**你不是还有…还有我吗？

**Huohuo:** Tail...?

> **藿藿：**你？

**Tail:** See you on the other side, Huohuo!

> **尾巴：**再见啦，藿藿！

**Cirrus:** You wretch! What are you doing!? Get out... get out!

> **浮烟：**你…混蛋！你来捣什么乱！给我…滚出去！

*(Begin battle against Cirrus ×20)*

(Gameplay note: Huohuo, who had lost Tail, will provide support from the sidelines)

**Cirrus:** G—Get out!

> **浮烟：**你，你给我滚出去！

**Tail:** Hey Cirrus, why don't you try swallowing ME too, huh?

> **尾巴：**浮烟，为什么不试试把本大爷也吸收了？

**Cirrus:** Are you insane? Why are you helping humans!?

> **浮烟：**你疯了吗？为什么要帮助人类？！

**Tail:** Why? You need a reason? How about this one...

> **尾巴：**理由？需要理由吗？非要理由的话——

**Tail:** I'd rather help a human than a conniving simpleton like you!

> **尾巴：**那就是老子瞧不上你这个只会耍弄诡计的白痴！

> **浮烟：**呜啊啊啊啊啊——！！！

The unanticipated turmoil of the Heliobi Disaster turned in a rain of fire. Cirrus grasped the chance to possess a spiritfarer, only for the Ten-Lords Commission to subdue it. Tail, however, is nowhere to be seen...

### Talk with Huohuo

> *The dust has settled... quite literally. After a night full of twists, noise, and running around with the Judges, you've finally reached the end of this eerie tale. But, just like most horror movies you've seen, directors always like to leave hints for a sequel... You pray to the heavens that there won't be a sequel!*

**(Trailblazer):** (I wonder how Huohuo is doing... I should check up on her.)

### (Optional) Talk with Xueyi

*(Talk to Xueyi)*

**Xueyi:** The one possessing Huohuo... seems to have disappeared. I don't know if I should be happy or sad for her...

> **雪衣：**附身藿藿的那家伙…似乎消失了。吾不知道该替藿藿高兴还是难过……

**Xueyi:** Never mind. I've never been good at figuring out emotions.

> **雪衣：**算了，情感思考一向非吾所长。

**Xueyi:** Thank you for your assistance, Nameless. Reinforcements from the Ten-Lords Commission will be here soon, and Guinaifen will be sent to an apothecary.

> **雪衣：**感谢出手相助，无名客。十王司的增援很快会来，桂乃芬也会被送去医馆看护的。

- ▸ Your body seems to be severely malfunctioning.

**Xueyi:** It is only an artificial ingenium body. I can swap into another one easily. Do not worry.

> **雪衣：**机巧假身，更换轻而易举，不必在意。

- ▸ I don't see Judge Hanya anywhere...

**Xueyi:** Hanya returned to the Ten-Lords Commission after you left. She is coming with the reinforcements as we speak.

> **雪衣：**…汝等离开后，寒鸦回十王司复命；如今她正带着增援赶来。

- ▸ Is Cirrus dead?

**Xueyi:** No. The demon lives on. They even possessed a young spiritfarer under my command. "As Starfire Essences, the heliobi will never die" — the records in the Ten-Lords Commission's evil-binding tomes were all real...

> **雪衣：**不，这邪物尚在，还劫持了吾麾下的冥差童子。身为星火之精，岁阳不会死灭，十王司缚魔典籍中的记载果然是真的。

**Xueyi:** Cirrus lost most of their power and poses no danger for the time being. But they will remain a threat for as long as they exist...

> **雪衣：**不过，这厮失去了它敛聚的大部分力量，暂时不足为惧。可它一日存在，就有一日的隐患……

**Xueyi:** Thankfully, the Ten-Lords Commission had prepared other sacred vessels to suppress the heliobi. We will construct a matrix all over this abandoned garden and seal the heliobi within. The Xianzhou will live to see another day.

> **雪衣：**所幸十王司已备妥了镇伏岁阳的法器，届时将以此处废园结成阵法，囚缚岁阳。今日的仙舟，算是渡过一劫了。

**Xueyi:** We can leave the rest to the Ten-Lords Commission from here... Even I feel like I need a rest.

> **雪衣：**接下来就交给十王司吧，吾…也想好好休息一番了。

*(Talk to Xueyi, optional)*

**Xueyi:** We can leave the rest to the Ten-Lords Commission from here... Even I feel like I need a rest.

### (Optional) Talk with Cirrus

*(Talk to the Possessed Spiritfarer)*

**Possessed Spiritfarer:** ...Grahhhh, don't even think about sealing me in the matrix!

**Possessed Spiritfarer:** You wretched fools! Tail, the judges, you... One day...

**Possessed Spiritfarer:** ...one day, sooner or later, I'll tear you all to pieces. I'll crunch your bones to dust, sap every drop of your strength, and spit you out into the dirt where you belong...

- ▸ ...Cirrus?

**Cirrus:** Already forgetting me?

> **浮烟：**这么快就认不出老朋友了？

- ▸ Wanna hug, sweety?

> **浮烟：**论恶毒，人类比起岁阳真是丝毫不逊色。

- ▸ I hope you repent.

**Cirrus:** Heliobi are faced with only a single punishment — eternal imprisonment. They can lock me up, but they can't kill me.

> **浮烟：**岁阳只会被判处一种刑罚，永远关押。他们杀不死我，却也不可能放走我。

- ▸ Leave the kid alone.

**Cirrus:** Kid? As a spiritfarer, he's already experienced more than you could in a lifetime.

> **浮烟：**孩子？身为冥差，他经历的人生要比你多的多。

**Cirrus:** For as long as I'm sealed in the matrix, I will inhabit this body. The judges can stick as many talismans and pour as much enchanted water on this flesh as they like — I don't care. As long as they want to save their own, I still have a bargaining chip.

> **浮烟：**只要我一天被困在此处，我就不会离开他的肉身。判官们尽可以给这具身体贴上神咒，涂抹符水，没关系，只要十王司还想保住属僚的性命，我就有讨价还价的余地。

- ▸ What tricks can you possibly pull as you are now?

**Cirrus:** There are many, (Trailblazer)... I will exist forever.

> **浮烟：**我能做的还有很多，开拓者，我会一直存在。

**Cirrus:** I have lived for countless years. I existed before the Alliance was even an idea. And once the Alliance has turned to dust, I will continue to exist for countless more Amber Eras.

> **浮烟：**联盟成立前，我已活了无数岁月，联盟消逝后，我仍将存在无数个琥珀纪。

**Cirrus:** I can wait. I'll wait for humans to let their guard down. I'll wait till the matrix drawn over Fyxestroll Garden collapses. Time and opportunity will always be on my side.

> **浮烟：**我能等待，等你们松懈，等绥园的阵法崩毁。时间和机会永远站在我这儿。

**Cirrus:** ...We will meet again.

> **浮烟：**是啊，咱们会再见面的。

**Cirrus:** I wasn't the only heliobus to escape when the Creation Furnace was destroyed... there are more of us than the mere few in Fyxestroll Garden. Where are they now? And what will they do to the Luofu?

> **浮烟：**除了绥园里咱们几个，造化洪炉破碎时，还有其他岁阳逃逸无踪。他们如今何在？又会在罗浮各处掀起什么水花来？

**Cirrus:** I look forward to seeing how the Ten-Lords Commission responds in the coming days...

> **浮烟：**我很期待十王司手忙脚乱的样子。

Cirrus stares at you in silence, and you can tell from their eyes that they are conspiring.

*(Talk to the Possessed Spiritfarer, optional)*

Cirrus stares at you in silence, and you can tell from their eyes that they are conspiring.

*(Talk to Huohuo)*

**Huohuo:** Tail... Mr. Tail...

**Huohuo:** Tail distracted Cirrus... He helped us so much... B—But now he's gone...

> **藿藿：**尾巴干扰了浮烟，是他帮了咱们…但是…但是他却不在了……

**Huohuo:** I can't sense him anywhere... He's gone.

> **藿藿：**我哪儿也感应不到他…他不在了。

- ▸ Huohuo...

**Huohuo:** What kind of judge am I...

> **藿藿：**…我真的是判官吗？

**Huohuo:** If I were stronger, Mr. Tail wouldn't have had to sacrifice himself...

> **藿藿：**要是我能再有用一些，尾巴就不至于牺牲自己……

**Huohuo:** It was because of him that I ended up in this terrible job...

> **藿藿：**虽然因为尾巴，我一直干着难熬的工作，过着苦哈哈的日子……

**Huohuo:** ...and I've thought so much about how much better my life would be if I never met him... but now that he's actually gone... I feel so empty inside...

> **藿藿：**有无数次想过要是没有这家伙，自己现在过的该有多舒坦啊，可是…可是真到了离开他的时刻…我…我心里却空落落的。

**Huohuo:** I really am useless... I've been looking forward to this day for so long, but now that it's here... I don't feel happy at all.

> **藿藿：**我果然是个不争气的人，自己期待已久的事情发生了，却一点也笑不出来。

**Huohuo:** Wait, you're right... Madam Xueyi said that heliobi are eternal flames that will never burn away. Mr. Tail... He must still be out there somewhere.

> **藿藿：**雪衣大人说，岁阳是不灭的火焰，尾巴大爷他…肯定就在某处。

**Huohuo:** ...I guess he is...

> **藿藿：**是啊，他自由了。

> **藿藿：**开拓者先生/小姐不用担心我。藿藿没事，我还得振作起来，继续执行任务呢。

**Huohuo:** Oh, the Ten-Lords Commission contacted Miss Guinaifen's friend — Miss Sushang. I can't leave my post, so... could you let Miss Sushang know what happened...? She'll be relieved.

> **藿藿：**对了，十王司联系了桂乃芬小姐的朋友素裳。我现在走不开，只好麻烦你去向素裳小姐解释解释，好让她放下心来。

### Go to Aurum Alley and talk with Sushang

> *This has now all come to an end, for the time being. Huohuo is worried about Guinaifen, and she hopes you can pass the news on soon to Guinaifen's bestie, Sushang. Not going with Guinaifen to Fyxestroll Garden may have been the correct decision for Sushang to make, but it may also be one that she might come to regret... Anyway, let's go have a chat.*

**Sushang:** Little Gui kinda gave me an outline of what happened in the apothecary... Can you tell me more?

> **素裳：**事情的大致，我已经在医馆里听小桂子讲过了。方便的话，能麻烦你再给我讲讲吗？

**You explain what happened in Fyxestroll Garden to Sushang, and she seems to realize the seriousness of the situation:**

**Sushang:** Thanks for telling me everything. I didn't think things would get so out of hand...

**Sushang:** I would've been able to help you if I were there too... and maybe Little Gui wouldn't have ended up in the apothecary... *sigh*

> **素裳：**要是我也在绥园里就能帮上你们了，说不定小桂子也不必住进医馆…哎。

**Sushang:** You don't need to worry about Little Gui. She won't be able to sit still, even in a sick ward. I'm sure she'll come find you again in no time.

> **素裳：**对了，你不必担心小桂子，她在病床上也不安生，也许过不了多久就来联系你了。

**Sushang:** I'm heading to the apothecary to check up on her. Catch you later.

> **素裳：**我正打算再去医馆看看她，我们就此别过吧。

*(Mission completed)*

(Obtain Achievement The Dangers in My Heart)

## 005　Fyxestroll Gathering (I)　绥园聚首•其一

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Fyxestroll Garden　|　角色：Trailblazer; Guinaifen; Sushang; Huohuo; Hanya; Cirrus

### Receive a message from Guinaifen

【游戏内短信 · Hey, do you have time right now?...】

**Guinaifen:** Hey, do you have time right now? Can we meet up at Fyxestroll Garden?

- ▸ Are you all right?

**(Trailblazer):** What are you planning to do at Fyxestroll Garden? By the way, are you feeling better now?

**Guinaifen:** Aw, thanks for the concern! People like me, who have to hustle all day out there under the sun, are all pretty tough...

**Guinaifen:** The healers in the Alchemy Commission have looked me over. Besides a bit of shock and some bruises, I'm totally fine!

- ▸ Are you the real Guinaifen?

**(Trailblazer):** Isn't Guinaifen supposed to be at the apothecary right now? Don't think you can fool me again, you little heliobus!

**Guinaifen:** Come on, can't we have some trust between us?

**Guinaifen:** The healers in the Alchemy Commission have looked me over. Besides a bit of shock and some bruises, I'm totally fine!

- ▸ Fyxestroll Garden again?

**(Trailblazer):** Didn't you cause a lot of trouble last time at Fyxestroll Garden?

**Guinaifen:** I'm terribly sorry for the trouble I caused last time.

**Guinaifen:** But I really need your help this time.

> **桂乃芬：**家人，有空吗？跑绥园一趟可以不？

**Guinaifen:** Actually, Hanya personally invited us for a meeting.

> **桂乃芬：**这回是来自寒鸦小姐发出邀请，召集咱们几个有关人士碰碰头

**Guinaifen:** Well, why don't you just come to Fyxestroll Garden so we can talk in person? Texting is too much of a hassle.

> **桂乃芬：**好啦，快点来绥园吧，打字太麻烦了，咱们当面再细聊

*{Accepted Mission: Fyxestroll Gathering (I)}*

### Meet up with Guinaifen in Fyxestroll Garden

> *The harrowing adventure in Fyxestroll Garden is an experience you hesitate to recount, but you simply cannot escape the mischievous hand of fate. Your next adventure begins with your blind faith in Little Gui's message, just as it did before.*

*(Talk to Cirrus, optional)*

**Cirrus:** This boredom is killing me... the only entertainment I have around here is watching the insects fly by.

**Cirrus:** Speaking of which, it seems one of them has come to visit me. I wonder, is he/she here to taunt me, or implement some sort of restorative justice? We'll just have to wait and see...

- ▸ Still haven't given up?

**Cirrus:** The Ten-Lords Commission judges won't abandon one of their own... but they won't let me off the hook either.

**Cirrus:** So now I'm stuck here, condemned to eternal boredom. Hehe, I wonder which will last longer — the lifespan of my jailors, or the patience of a heliobus...

**Cirrus:** In any case, time is on my side, so long as the Xianzhou are willing to maintain some facade of humanity.

- ▸ What are your terms of surrender?

**Cirrus:** Surrender? Why would I do that? To you, I may as well be fighting an unwinnable battle — wasting my time away in someone else's body...

**Cirrus:** ...but for me, the game has only just begun. The Ten-Lords Commission doesn't have any moves left... and I still have time to turn the game in my favor.

**Cirrus:** You know, it wasn't that long ago that you and those judges would have become my pawns — if it wasn't for that meddling guard dog...

- ▸ I'm speechless.

**Cirrus:** If you have nothing to say, then run along and let me be.

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Guinaifen:** Yo, what's up! You're finally here! Without you, our ghost-hunting squad would be nothing!

> **桂乃芬：**家人，你终于来了！要是缺了你，咱们这支「捉鬼小队」可就缺了一个强大战力！

**Sushang:** Hey, I'm a reliable squad member too!

> **素裳：**论战斗，我也是很靠谱的啊！

**Guinaifen:** Sure, when you're not passed out in fear! Hehe...

> **桂乃芬：**嘿嘿，是啊，裳裳你醒着的时候最靠谱了！

**Sushang:** Cut it out — if you keep teasing me, I—I... I'm not gonna perform my boulder-smashing trick for you anymore!

> **素裳：**小桂子，你要是再讽刺我…我、我就再也不配合你表演胸口碎大石了！

- ▸ What's this ghost-hunting squad all about?

**Hanya:** The official name is the "Ten-Lords Commission Special Operations Team for the Heliobi Containment Breach Incident."

> **寒鸦：**记录在案正确的名称应当是「十王司下辖岁阳逃逸事件特别处理行动小组」。

**Hanya:** That was the name given to us by decree of the Ten Lords. Since we managed to survive the Fyxestroll Garden incident, the Ten Lords decided to entrust us with hunting down and subduing the heliobi using our special skills.

> **寒鸦：**这是由十王颁旨所成立的队伍名目。想邀请前不久绥园灾异事件后安然无恙的几位，以各位最擅长的方式寻找并镇伏岁阳。

- ▸ By decree of the Ten Lords?

**Hanya:** That's right — this expedition was commissioned by the Ten Lords themselves. I am unsure as to why they attached so much importance to this endeavor, but I have a theory.

> **寒鸦：**嗯，此事是由十王亲自下诏促成。虽然我并没有得到更多理由，但多半也能猜到其中的原因。

- ▸ Using our special skills?

**Sushang:** Huohuo is a judge of the Ten-Lords Commission and I'm a Cloud Knight — we must answer the call to the best of our abilities. And (Trailblazer) is an outworlder who's done great things for the sake of the Xianzhou...

> **素裳：**藿藿是判官，我是云骑，十王司有调遣责无旁贷。开拓者嘛，也是为罗浮出过力流过汗的天外友人……

**Sushang:** But why did Little Gui have to get dragged into this? This mission is too dangerous for a civilian!

> **素裳：**但怎么把小桂子这样的群众也给拉下水了？这多危险！

**Guinaifen:** ...I get it. You don't think I have any special skills, so I'll just get in the way?

> **桂乃芬：**…我明白了，你是嫌我帮不上忙，只会给你们添乱是吧？

**Hanya:** Every flower and tree on the Xianzhou is significant in its own way — Miss Gui has her own strengths too. As a famous street performer and livestreamer, she has precisely the skills we need to acquire intel for our mission.

> **寒鸦：**仙舟上一花一草皆有意义，桂小姐自然有她的过人之处：身为抛头露面的街头艺人，网络上的主播，她非常清楚如何扩展咱们需要的消息来源。

- ▸ I don't have any more questions.

**Hanya:** Miss Gui has set up a social media account under the guise of investigating paranormal phenomena and unexplained mysteries... we can use it to gather intel for our mission.

> **寒鸦：**我已劳烦桂小姐在某个网站上设立了账号，以解决诡事奇闻为由头，为各位行动提供信源。

**Guinaifen:** You hear that, Sushang! I'm a key member of the squad! They said I could be a Ten-Lords Commission intelligence hawk!

> **桂乃芬：**裳裳，听见了吗！这回的我岂止是有用，简直是有用。能有机会作为十王司的鹰犬帮上大家的忙，真是太好了！

**Sushang:** Uh... I might not be a scholar, but I don't think calling someone a "hawk" is a compliment.

> **素裳：**呃，虽然我没什么文化，但「鹰犬」不是什么好词儿吧？

**Hanya:** The Ten-Lords Commission has specially entrusted Judge Huohuo with commanding the ghost-hunting squad, so please make sure to follow her lead in all matters related to the heliobi.

> **寒鸦：**判官藿藿受命调遣各位，在岁阳相关的专业问题上，请你们务必配合她的指挥。

**Huohuo:** ...Th—That's right. I won't let us down.

> **藿藿：**我…我明白了，藿藿一定会不辱使命的。

**After Hanya delivered the task to the ghost-hunting squad, she left to continue resolving matters at Fyxestroll Garden.:**

### Talk to Huohuo and learn about the task commissioned by the Ten-Lords Commission.

> *When you arrive at Fyxestroll Garden, a ghost-hunting squad is already assembled — organized under the grace of the Ten-Lords Commission, no less. Guinaifen intends to find information about escaped heliobi using the most prominent website on supernatural phenomena on the Xianzhou Luofu. Could this be the beginning of yet another tumultuous adventure?*

> ▸ 万一只是谣言怎么办？

**Huohuo:** Thank you... if we're ever going to find Tail, now's the best chance we have.

> **藿藿：**网站上一直有人在分享自己的灵异经历，我之前也常去那上面看「见鬼实录」来锻炼胆量……

- ▸ He might not be around anymore.

> ▸ 装神弄鬼多半都是人为的。

**Huohuo:** Mm... I guess so. Maybe he ran away, or broke into tiny pieces... Either way, I'd like to know what happened to him.

> **藿藿：**我们想借这个网站获取信息，再打着解决灵异事件的幌子暗中调查。

**Huohuo:** O—Of course, finding Tail isn't our top priority right now — we need to catch and subdue all the fleeing escaped heliobi as soon as possible.

> **藿藿：**毕竟冥差大张旗鼓挨家挨户的搜索，不但效率低下，还会引起人们惊慌，激化岁阳对宿主的控制。

*(Talk to Huohuo again, optional)*

**Huohuo:** Tail... The important thing right now is stopping the heliobi from causing chaos.

> **藿藿：**嗯，不止咱们几个，十王司的冥差们也会暗行调查。

*(Talk to Hanya, optional)*

**Hanya:** Mr./Miss (Trailblazer), it is good to see you are doing well.

> **寒鸦：**开拓者先生/小姐，见到您一切安好，甚是可喜。

**Hanya:** During the Fyxestroll Garden incident, my sister gave the order to seek reinforcements. It was thanks to your support in that critical moment that we were able to get the situation under control. I wanted to thank you.

> **寒鸦：**绥园灾异一事，我受姐姐命令前去请回援助。在此期间，多亏了您在园中撑持，才令妖祸不致泛滥，小女子须向您致谢。

**Hanya:** And now this small team needs your help, too.

> **寒鸦：**而今这支小队伍，也需要您的帮助。

- ▸ Where's Xueyi?

> ▸ 雪衣怎么不在？

**Hanya:** She sustained heavy injuries in the previous incident.

> **寒鸦：**姐姐在之前的战斗中，损伤甚重。

**Hanya:** Fortunately, my sister is using an ingenium body. As long as she returns to the coffin and is tended to by the spiritfarers, her life is not in jeopardy. I still worry about her, though.

> **寒鸦：**所幸姐姐是以机巧之身还阳行事。若回到棺中，由冥差们维护更新，不至于有损性命。但我依然很担心她。

**Hanya:** Creatures of flesh and blood fear death and will always strive to preserve their life... but not my sister. Her human instinct for survival is slipping away — she sees herself more as a "tool," driving her body to the point of total exhaustion.

> **寒鸦：**血肉之躯会畏惧死伤，想要保全自己，但姐姐不会。她正渐渐脱离自己为人时的常识，将自己视为「工具」，毫不体恤地消耗自己的身体。

**Hanya:** Every time she fights like that, I can't bear to watch.

> **寒鸦：**每当看到她战斗，我都深感不忍。

- ▸ Can we really handle this better than the Ten-Lords Commission?

> ▸ 我们真的能比十王司更专业？

**Hanya:** In most matters, I would say no. But when dealing with heliobi... I think the answer is quite clear.

> **寒鸦：**在其他事情上也许未必，但在岁阳一事上，答案已经很明了了。

**Hanya:** The Ten-Lords Commission have long specialized in suppressing abominations, but they were negligent in guarding against the dangers of the Creation Furnace. Once it became a problem, the spiritfarers and aurumatons were powerless against the heliobi, proving that the traditional means of subduing them were no longer effective.

> **寒鸦：**罗浮十王司长久以来专攻镇伏孽物，却对洪炉的危险疏于防范。此次变生腋肘，冥差与金人对岁阳无力拮抗，证明过去的伏魔手段已经不奏效了。

**Hanya:** As fate would have it, the girl we unwittingly rescued many years ago became the key to defeating Cirrus.

> **寒鸦：**反倒是许多年前我们无意间救下的女孩，成了击败浮烟的关键。世事真是奇妙。

**Hanya:** After this is all settled, we intend to make contact with the Xianzhou Zhuming. That ship is under the influence of the mythical Reignbow Arbiter — the people there are able to coexist with the heliobi and at the same time be free from their manipulation. We hope that from them we can learn the art of breaking free from the influence of the heliobi.

> **寒鸦：**此事之后，我们打算与朱明仙舟通信。那座仙舟受帝弓神话的影响，人们与岁阳共生，又能不受其左右。吾等当能从那儿求得抵御岁阳之术。

- ▸ Is it true that the Ten-Lords Commission is located in... the netherworld?

> ▸ 十王司真的在…阴间？

**Hanya:** Did you really think we were messengers from another plane visiting the world of the living? Hehe, how cute of you.

> **寒鸦：**您真把我们当成与阳世不通音信的幽冥来客么？这份天真可爱得紧。

**Hanya:** And maybe we are... don't get too close, or you might turn into a ghost too.

> **寒鸦：**就当是吧。与小女子靠得太近，会有损阳气喔。

**Hanya:** The precise location of the Ten-Lords Commission is highly confidential... but you have contributed much to the Xianzhou and are very close to the secret. If you wish to know more about it, I will have to ask you to spend time with our ghost-hunting squad.

> **寒鸦：**十王司所在之地，是绝对的秘密。您为仙舟贡献良多，离这个秘密已然很近了。若想进一步知道，「捉鬼小队」的事情，要劳您多多费心了。

- ▸ About Huohuo and Tail...

> ▸ 藿藿和尾巴…

**Hanya:** A judge of the Ten-Lords Commission has no past. Once one has entered the service of the commission, all links with the outside world are severed...

> **寒鸦：**十王司判官的过去原是一律被抹去的，一入幽府，便不牵外缘了。

**Hanya:** However, since you are now a member of the squad, and in the interest of everyone working together, it's best that we know each other a little better... — I'll tell you some of the secrets I know.

> **寒鸦：**不过，你眼下已是这支小队的一员了，若要彼此合作，那么了解自然是少不得的一环。我就和你谈谈自己所知的一些秘辛旧事好了。

**Hanya:** After Ignamar was defeated by the general in a great battle, the Ten-Lords Commission was ordered to seal away the heliobi fragments. To make them atone for their actions, most of the heliobi were sealed inside the Creation Furnace.

> **寒鸦：**自从「燎原」与将军大战落败后，十王司受命封印分灵碎片。大部分岁阳都被收入造化洪炉，为其贻害而赎罪。

**Hanya:** Decades ago, the Ten-Lords Commission was asked to investigate an unusual case — a Foxian girl's tail had been consumed by a strange fire of unknown origin... so we set out, ready to subdue whatever foul demon awaited us.

> **寒鸦：**数十年前，十王司收到请求，有个狐人小姑娘的尾巴被一团无源怪火给吞噬了，于是我等奉命出巡，镇伏妖物。

**Hanya:** It turned out that the fire was none other than a heliobus. The remaining fragments of Ignamar — the heliobi — had found some way to escape from their furnace prison.

> **寒鸦：**待我们赶到，才察觉这团火正是逃逸在外的岁阳。不知为何，「燎原」的分灵碎片中仍有漏网之鱼。

**Hanya:** That heliobus's power was weakened to the brink of death, but he was kept alive thanks to the kindness of the Foxian girl. Gradually, he regained his strength and rekindled his desire to manipulate humans.

> **寒鸦：**它的力量本已衰弱之极，却因小姑娘的一念之善而被留存了下来。它渐渐恢复了心智，重又燃起了试图操控人类的念头。

**Hanya:** Normally, I would have just purged the heliobus and sent it back into the furnace. But Huohuo's body was too frail and weak to perform a purging. The best I could do without harming her was to inscribe a talisman seal — enough to subdue the thing living inside her, at least for a while.

> **寒鸦：**我本该将岁阳连根祓除，送入洪炉。可惜那时藿藿的身子骨太过衰弱，为了能保住她的生命，我只能书写符敕，将那东西暂时镇伏在她体内。

**Hanya:** The Ten-Lords Commission took Huohuo in as a spiritfarer apprentice, teaching her the arts of purgation and ghost hunting. This way the Commission could keep an eye on Huohuo — and ensure that she wasn't taken over by the heliobus.

> **寒鸦：**之后十王司将藿藿招募为冥差学徒，学习驱魔捉鬼的本事，这不仅是为了看护藿藿，也是为了时刻观察她是否有被夺舍的情况。

- ▸ But Tail doesn't seem like an enemy.

> ▸ 但是尾巴不太像敌人。

**Hanya:** Perhaps all those long years with Huohuo have bestowed upon that heliobus some form of human compassion. Though Tail has never given up on attempting to break free of his bonds or usurping his host, he has fought against his own kind to protect Huohuo.

> **寒鸦：**也许这些年与藿藿漫长的相处，让它学会了人类怜惜的情感。虽然尾巴一直没有放弃过挣脱束缚或篡噬寄主，但至少它为了藿藿对抗了同族。

- ▸ Tail betrayed his own kind.

> ▸ 尾巴却背叛了同族。

**Hanya:** You could say that. Who would have thought Tail would have sacrificed himself to defeat Cirrus? Granted, heliobi have no obligation to be loyal to one another, but Tail's willingness to side with Huohuo is significant.

> **寒鸦：**是啊，谁能想到尾巴牺牲自己战胜了浮烟呢？虽说岁阳之间没有同心同德的必然，但他愿意站在藿藿这边，已经说明了很多事情。

- ▸ This has to be the strangest group of people on the Xianzhou...

> ▸ 明白，我就是仙舟奇兵队长。

**Hanya:** I would save the jokes for later! As the saying goes, with great work comes great rewards. The Ten-Lords Commission will compensate you accordingly.

> **寒鸦：**您说笑了。所谓能者多劳，多劳多得，此番盟助，十王司也会重重酬谢。

**Hanya:** I've wondered at times what the Luofu should give the Nameless in return for their great deeds...

> **寒鸦：**小女子时常也在想，罗浮欠下无名客如许恩情，该拿什么来回报？

**Hanya:** Alas, questions like that are above my pay grade. That is for the Ten Lords and the General to figure out.

> **寒鸦：**思来想去，实在没有答案。毕竟这可是十王与将军才能操心的事情。

*(Talk to Hanya again)*

**Hanya:** I leave the matters of the ghost-hunting squad in your hands. If you have further questions, just ask Huohuo.

> **寒鸦：**「捉鬼小队」的事情，要劳您多多费心了。若有什么疑问，藿藿可代小女子解答。

**Hanya:** As always, I have mountains of paperwork to finish... Piles of paper... I'll never get it all done...

> **寒鸦：**小女子身为文职，要操劳案牍之事去了。唉唉，做不完，真的做不完啊……

## 006　Twin Bond　双生

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Fyxestroll Garden　|　角色：Trailblazer; Guinaifen; Sushang; Huohuo; Cirrus; Jing Yuan; Bailu; Luxie　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''Fyxestroll Gathering (I)'' completed

### Get information out of Cirrus.

> *Since you have already captured a heliobus criminal, you decide to get as much value out of it as possible. You will try to flip it so as to provide evidence for you.*

*(Talk to Cirrus)*

**Cirrus:** Ugh, this tedium... even the insects can't hold my attention any longer. I wonder if these ones will prove to be more entertaining?

> **浮烟：**啊，无聊到令人心碎，连偶尔有飞虫掠过都成了乐趣。但这一次，有几只小飞虫一块找上门来了……

**Sushang:** Cirrus, we've come to ask you a few questions. I hope you'll be cooperative.

> **素裳：**浮烟，想找你了解点情况，请你配合一下我们的工作。

**Cirrus:** Oh, if it isn't the little girl who fainted in Fyxestroll Garden the moment she encountered the heliobi? Already talking to me like a detective, are you?

> **浮烟：**哟，这不是一进绥园就昏了过去的小妹妹吗？几天不见，说话口气就硬成这样了？

**Sushang:** Hey! Where's my sword? Y—You just wait...

> **素裳：**嘿！我的剑呢？你你你给我等着……

- ▸ Can we destroy Cirrus already?

**Cirrus:** Hmph, there are plenty of beings in this garden that would like to kill me... Why don't you line up one-by-one and take turns trying?

> **浮烟：**嗯哼，这园子里想拿我开刀的人多了去了，要不你们商量商量排个先后？

**Huohuo:** D—Don't be ridiculous... We can't allow the host to be harmed.

> **藿藿：**不、不可以…宿主是无辜的。

- ▸ Don't let them get to you.

**Sushang:** Okay, okay... I just wish I could teach them a lesson!

> **素裳：**你说得对。忍一时风平浪静…退一步越想越气…

**Huohuo:** Cirrus... you must be unhappy with how things turned out, right?

> **藿藿：**浮烟…因为一个小小的疏忽落败了，你…很不甘心吧？

**Cirrus:** What do you want from me, little fox girl? You want to know what happened to the guard dog? He turned to ashes a long time ago!

> **浮烟：**小狐狸，你想从我这儿得到什么？想知道那条看门狗的下落？抱歉，它早就灰•飞•烟•灭啦！

**Huohuo:** I... I don't care about that at all... but I can't help but notice — you managed to escape the furnace, and now you're stuck here, unable to move an inch, while the other heliobi are roaming around the Luofu enjoying themselves... Must be quite frustrating.

> **藿藿：**我…不关心这些。倒是你…好不容易从洪炉里逃了出来。眼下有的岁阳在罗浮各处逍遥快活。你却要被困在这儿寸步难行…你甘心吗？

**Cirrus:** Hahaha... You want me to assist you and betray my own kind?

> **浮烟：**哈哈哈…你想要我帮助你们，出卖同类？

Cirrus stays silent, sizing everyone up through the eyes of the spiritfarer they possess. Finally, they decide to talk.

**Cirrus:** ...I can do that — under one condition... I wish to arrange a challenge — one akin to the struggle between the great heliobus Ignamar and the general of the Luofu. As long as you can bring me the current general... anything can be negotiated.

> **浮烟：**我有一个条件。我想重续当年大岁阳「燎原」与将军的胜负。只要你能把如今罗浮的将军带到我面前…一切都有可谈的余地。

**Huohuo:** The general...?

> **藿藿：**将军……？

**Cirrus:** What, is that too much to ask? It seems the judge here doesn't have what it takes to negotiate with a heliobus!

> **浮烟：**怎么，这就难倒你了？看来判官大人想和岁阳做交易的决心，还•不•够•啊！

**Guinaifen:** That's just another way of saying they don't want to cooperate...

> **桂乃芬：**这家伙张口说的每个字都是在变着法拒绝你……

**Guinaifen:** Huh? Wait, Huohuo, you can't seriously be thinking about—

> **桂乃芬：**哎？藿藿，你不会真的打算……

**Huohuo:** Deal! I'll submit a request to the Seat of Divine Foresight.

> **藿藿：**成交，我会去神策府请将军来。

*(Talk to Cirrus, optional)*

**Cirrus:** Bring me the current general of the Luofu, and we can talk.

> **浮烟：**把如今罗浮的将军带到我面前，咱们或许可以谈谈。

### Head to the Seat of Divine Foresight and ask for Jing Yuan's personal assistance.

> *Since you have already captured a heliobus criminal, you decide to get as much value out of it as possible. You will try to flip it so as to provide evidence for you. Unexpectedly, Cirrus takes the opportunity to present terms. It wants to duel with the general of the Luofu to consume the latter's mind.*

**Jing Yuan:** Oh? What do we have here? A Nameless, a Cloud Knight, a Judge of the Ten-Lords Commission, and a street performer? Quite the group.

> **景元：**嗯？无名客、云骑、十王司判官还有街头艺人…这个组合倒是新奇。

**Guinaifen:** Wow, the general recognizes me? I didn't realize I was so famous!

> **桂乃芬：**呀，将军大人居然认得我吗？没想到我竟然这么有名气了！

**Jing Yuan:** Haha, my retainer, Yanqing, has mentioned you. You are one of the only people on the Luofu that was able to deprive him of his sword.

> **景元：**哈哈，听我的侍卫彦卿提过。你可是罗浮上少有能令他失去佩剑，空手而返的人。

**Guinaifen:** Huh? Oh! I—I remember... I should apologize — I did a sword-swallowing performance on the spur of the moment, but forgot to return the sword to him when I left the stage...

> **桂乃芬：**我…我想起来了！真是对不住对不住。一时兴起表演了吞剑，结果散场时竟然忘了把那柄剑还给小哥。

**Jing Yuan:** So, what wind has brought you to the Seat of Divine Foresight?

> **景元：**所以，今天吹的是什么风，让几位聚到神策府来找我？

Huohuo tells Jing Yuan about the heliobi crisis and the Cirrus's demand.

**Huohuo:** General, we know you have important matters to attend to, and the Ten-Lords Commission shouldn't be bothering you with something as trivial as the heliobi... but if we allow those creatures to roam freely, I'm afraid...

> **藿藿：**将军日理万机，十王司本不该拿岁阳的事情来打扰您。但任由那些妖物到处徘徊，恐怕……

**Jing Yuan:** I heard stories of a skirmish between a previous general and a monster of sorts — I'd always assumed they were an old wives tale. I never imagined the villain of such tales would come to my doorstep seeking revenge. I must say, it seems a bit unfair that I should assume the debt for a conflict I had no involvement in.

> **景元：**嗯，我曾听闻过前代将军与精怪交战的逸话，还以为是随口编成的故事。没想到正主寻仇上门来了，只是找到我头上却有些冤枉了。

- ▸ It wasn't your fight to begin with.

**Jing Yuan:** Precisely! In principle, the instigator should bear the burden. Still...

> **景元：**话说的不错！本着「谁污染，谁治理」的原则确该让当事人出马。但是……

- ▸ Masterstroke them! Please?

**Jing Yuan:** It would appear you are eager to see me assume the debt.

> **景元：**看来开拓者是铁了心要我奉还人情了。

**Jing Yuan:** General Teng Xiao is a venerated predecessor. If I am to honor his legacy, then sitting idly by is not an option. If an audience with the general of the Luofu is requested, then I will honor such a request.

> **景元：**腾骁将军是我敬重的前辈，我克绍箕裘，自然不能旁观坐视。人家指明了要见罗浮的将军，那我就会会它。

**Huohuo:** General, heliobi are vile creatures capable of taking over the bodies of mortals... If you're unsure...

> **藿藿：**将军，岁阳这邪物极其擅长占夺凡人的肉体，如果您有顾虑……

**Jing Yuan:** I appreciate the Ten-Lords Commission having such high regard for my welfare. It is true I am rather inexperienced when it comes to heliobi, but as general of the Luofu, I cannot look on while these fugitive creatures wreak havoc on the populace.

> **景元：**多谢十王司为我考虑。我虽不擅长对付妖魔鬼怪，但岁阳逃逸，祸及仙舟黎庶，身为将军的我责无旁贷。

**Jing Yuan:** I still have some paperwork that needs attending to — once I am finished, I will make my way to Fyxestroll Garden without delay.

> **景元：**几位不妨先行一步，待批了手头的公文，我会立刻前往绥园。

*(Talk to Jing Yuan, optional)*

**Jing Yuan:** As soon as I finish my paperwork for the day, I will make my way to Fyxestroll Garden. Please, go ahead of me.

> **景元：**几位不妨先行一步，待批阅完手头的公文，我会立刻前往绥园。

### Return to Fyxestroll Garden and talk to Cirrus.

> *Jing Yuan happily accepts Cirrus' proposition. The heliobus immediately draws a boundary line on the Verdant Terrace and makes a wager with you. If it emerges victorious, then Jing Yuan must fight it. However, if it fails, then it will provide you with information aiding your plan to capture all the heliobi.*

**Cirrus:** I have never seen this one before... Human, who are you?

> **浮烟：**我没在园子里见过你…人类，你是谁？

**Jing Yuan:** Heliobus, is it not your deepest wish to hold a rematch with the general of the Luofu? I have come to grant you an audience.

> **景元：**岁阳，你不是心心念念想要重续与将军的战斗么？我应邀而来。

- ▸ He's the present-day general!
- ▸ This is General Teng Xiao's successor!

**Huohuo:** Cirrus, we have brought you the person you wish to see. This is General Jing Yuan, the one who now sits at the Seat of Divine Foresight.

> **藿藿：**浮烟，你想见的人，我们已为你请来了。如今坐镇神策府的，便是这位景元将军。

**Cirrus:** ...This is the current general of the Luofu?

> **浮烟：**…这就是当代的将军？

**Cirrus:** Look at that expression — he's barely half-awake! Where is General Teng Xiao? Why did he choose such a lightweight to take over for him? I suppose it's already too late for the Xianzhou to rid themselves of this clown...

> **浮烟：**一副刚刚睡醒的样子，看起来弱爆了好嘛。腾骁呢？他怎么选了这么个家伙来接任？不过，让仙舟人比武招选，重新决出个将军来也不现实。

**Cirrus:** Very well then, General Jing Yuan — how about we settle our score right here in Fyxestroll Garden? If I win, I must be free to come and go as I please...

> **浮烟：**那么，景元将军，咱们就在这绥园里一决胜负如何？！若我获胜，就任我自由来去——

**Jing Yuan:** I agreed to grant you an audience, not meet you in combat.

> **景元：**我只说「应邀而来」，却从没答应要和你一战。

**Cirrus:** ...What? You refuse? You won't fight me!?

> **浮烟：**…什么，你不应战？你不应战？！

**Cirrus:** He comes here after I demand a rematch, and won't even fight... Is he conceding that I already won? No, there can be no victory in eschewing combat — human logic negates it...

> **浮烟：**他说为了重续当年的战斗而来到此处，却不应战，难道是变相承认我不战而胜？不对，在人类的逻辑里，不战和胜利不能划上等号……

**Jing Yuan:** You misunderstand — I came for a rematch. It's just that... you are not a worthy opponent.

> **景元：**你误会了，我确实是为当年之战而来，只是…你还不配。

**Cirrus:** WHAT!? UNWORTHY!?

> **浮烟：**你——说——什——么——!!

**Jing Yuan:** Teng Xiao, as general of the Luofu, was commander-in-chief of the Cloud Knights. Ignamar commanded the heliobi. The two were as great as the sky and the earth — equally matched like yin and yang. It is only natural that they were worthy opponents.

> **景元：**腾骁身为罗浮的将军，节制云骑；而燎原则是岁阳之首，号令群魔；二者如天覆地载，阴阳相薄，双方龙战实在是合情合理，合乎规矩。

**Jing Yuan:** As it stands, I am the general of the Luofu... but you are no Ignamar. Merely a prisoner. If you wish to engage me in battle, you must follow my rules. Defeat my army — only then will I grant you the fight you seek.

> **景元：**而眼下，我乃是仙舟的将军，你却已不再是燎原，你不过是一介系狱囚犯。要想与我交战，须得按我的规矩，击败我的军队，杀到中军帐前，我才给你这个机会。

**Cirrus:** ...Your army?

> **浮烟：**…你的军队？

**Jing Yuan:** That's right. You must defeat my four companions gathered here — then you will have proved yourself a worthy opponent.

> **景元：**没错，以此高台为限，你要击败我身后这四位朋友。

**Huohuo:** Wait... he means us!? W—W—W—We'll be okay, right?

> **藿藿：**哎？我们？没没没没问题…吗？

- ▸ We got this.

**Sushang:** That's right! For us, any problem that can be solved by force isn't a problem at all.

> **素裳：**是啊，能靠武力解决的问题都不是问题。

- ▸ That escalated quickly...

**Sushang:** We'll be fine! For us, any problem that can be solved by force isn't a problem at all.

> **素裳：**打就打呗，能靠武力解决的问题都不是问题。

> **桂乃芬：**我年纪轻轻，将军怎么对我这么有信心啊？

> **浮烟：**…只要打败这区区四个卒子？

**Jing Yuan:** It would serve you well not to underestimate them. They are as skilled in combat as they are tenacious in spirit. I worry they might pose a problem for you.

> **景元：**你可不要小看他们四人，这四位各个身怀绝技，斗志与耐性也是技惊四座。我只怕你拾掇不下。

**Jing Yuan:** If you win, you can have your rematch. If you lose, you will answer one question of their choice — no lies, no tricks. Can you accept these terms?

> **景元：**若你赢了，我便重续当年的对决。若是输了，你要回答他们一个问题——不可说谎，不可狡辩。成交？

**Cirrus:** ...

> **浮烟：**……

**Cirrus:** It's a deal! The game is set — this platform will be our chessboard, and the fighters will be the pieces!

> **浮烟：**成交！就让我们以此高台为枰，以人为子，来一番较量！

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Cirrus:** Hmph, trying to humiliate me with lowly pawns...

**Cirrus:** Receive my power!

*(After the battle)*

**Jing Yuan:** Should you decide to try again, my offer still stands... but you must defeat them.

> **景元：**胜败兵家事未期。若你想再来过，我可以随时奉陪，等候你不断挑战。当然，得赢过这四位才行。

**Cirrus:** Then what are we waiting for!

> **浮烟：**那就再来！

**Huohuo:** Not so fast! Before we fight again, you need to answer one question, like you promised.

> **藿藿：**且慢！在开始下一场战斗前，你要按约回答十王司一个问题。

**Cirrus:** Pesky devil of a girl... Alright, what's your question?

> **浮烟：**讨厌的小鬼…你问吧！

You show Cirrus the post about Bailu's sudden disappearance

**Cirrus:** There are countless heliobi that escaped from the Creation Furnace, and every one of them could have done such a thing. It could have been vengeance, curiosity, or simply hunger... So how would I know which one abducted your Dragon Lady?

> **浮烟：**被封禁在洪炉里的岁阳多得很，每个都有可能。出于报复，出于好奇，出于饥饿…我怎么知道会是哪个拐走了你们的龙女？

**Cirrus:** Of course, I do have my suspicions. For example, Luxie...

> **浮烟：**不过，怀疑的对象，倒也不是没有。比如阿灼……

- ▸ Sounds like a childish name for a heliobus.
- ▸ Sounds like a made-up name to me.

**Cirrus:** Disbelieve it if you like — that's up to you. Unlike humans, I get no joy from telling lies.

> **浮烟：**信不信由你，我可不像人类那般热爱谎言。

**Cirrus:** Luxie loves mischief. They give no thought to interfering in mortal affairs — their only concerns are entertainment and freedom. Among all the fragments of Ignamar, the one most likely to abduct your precious Dragon Lady... would be Luxie.

> **浮烟：**这家伙玩心极重，全不在意怎么操控凡人，一心只想着到处自由嬉戏。要说「燎原」分裂的碎片里，谁最可能干下诱拐你们家龙女的事情……也就只有它了。

**Cirrus:** We were sealed away for a thousand years — it's been ages since we last tasted the joys and sorrows of the world of humans. I'm sure Luxie was eager to find a new friend and wander the world with them.

> **浮烟：**封印千年至今，我们久久不曾品尝人类的喜怒哀乐，想必阿灼也一定迫不及待地到处溜达找个玩伴。

**Huohuo:** I think they're telling the truth... Heliobi do have different personalities, and they can be influenced by the personality of their host.

> **藿藿：**这坏东西的话多半是真的。岁阳的心性各不相同，全赖寄宿对象的性格熏染而成。

**Huohuo:** If a heliobus possesses a great warrior, they might gradually take on a braver, fiercer persona... and if their host is a playful child, they may adopt a childlike spirit.

> **藿藿：**若是依附了好斗的战士，性格便渐渐好勇斗狠了起来；而要是遇上了玩心甚重的孩童，也就成了童心未泯的游灵。

**Guinaifen:** Hmm... "wander the world with them." It sounds like Lady Bailu would've been an ideal target — she's always running off to play in Aurum Alley.

> **桂乃芬：**我瞧白露小姐经常逃家跑到金人巷来玩儿，感觉也是个渴望自由的小孩子啊。

**Huohuo:** Mm... This intel should be a big help.

> **藿藿：**嗯，有了这条线索，事情办起来想必也能顺利多了。

**Cirrus:** Hey! We're not done here! You still owe me a fight!

**Cirrus:** You bunch of meat bags! One minor victory and you think you're something special! Come on, show me what you've got!

> **浮烟：**混蛋，赢了区区一场就神气活现！再来，再来啊！

**Jing Yuan:** The clues are all pointing in the same direction now. It would be wise to strike while the iron is hot.

> **景元：**事情有了眉目，镇伏岁阳之事还要拜托诸位快快行动起来。

**Sushang:** Thank you for your help, general. Let's get going!

> **素裳：**多谢将军，咱们这就出发吧！

*(Talk to Cirrus, optional)*

**Cirrus:** Hah, spare me! Have you forgotten that I once controlled you like a puppet?

**Cirrus:** I provided you with information as promised — why am I still empty-handed?

> **浮烟：**我已按约为你们提供了线索，怎么还是空手而归？

**Cirrus:** Tsk, don't bother me again until you're ready to fight!

*(Talk to Jing Yuan, optional)*

**Jing Yuan:** The heliobi are like a flame that burns eternal, with no need for sustenance. But alas, their curiosity got the best of them, and they became prisoners of humans. It is a pity.

> **景元：**岁阳本是无根自在之火，可惜却因一念好奇，为人类所囿。可惜。

- ▸ Do you sympathize with them?

**Jing Yuan:** ...I lament that we share the same weaknesses.

> **景元：**与其说同情，不如说是关照。

**Jing Yuan:** Our situation is no different from that of the heliobi. From the moment we gain sentience, we are driven by desire and instinct, bound to our destiny, moving unceasingly towards our own demise.

> **景元：**我们与岁阳的处境并无不同，从拥有灵智的那一刻起，受欲念和本性驱使，注定要被束缚在自己的命途上，步向自己的结局。

- ▸ Aren't you afraid we'll be defeated by them...

**Jing Yuan:** The most important thing in a soldier is one's intuition for victory or defeat. And you have given me confidence in victory.

> **景元：**持节将兵，最重要的是对胜败的直觉。而各位令我有必胜的信心。

- ▸ Why is Cirrus so obsessed with fighting the general of the Luofu?

**Jing Yuan:** The ancient legends recount that the Xianzhou once faced an invasion by a great enemy that threatened the annihilation of every race aboard the Xianzhou ships. In order to save the Alliance, a hero made a pact with the Flint Emperor — the predecessor of all heliobi.

> **景元：**按古老的神话所载，仙舟曾面临大敌入侵，举族覆灭的绝境。为此，有位凡人英雄为了挽救危亡，不惜与岁阳之祖「燧皇」立下夺舍约定。

**Jing Yuan:** The Flint Emperor would aid the Xianzhou in battle, and if they were victorious, the hero and their followers would lose their physical bodies for eternity.

> **景元：**燧皇借出力量助战，而凡人与其追随者们若能凯旋，将会永远失去自己的身体。

**Jing Yuan:** When the fighting had ceased, none of the heroes survived, and the Flint Emperor was gravely wounded. Thus, the pact was unable to be fulfilled. As the centuries came and went, the story faded from memory. But the heliobi remember.

> **景元：**那场大战结束后，英雄们无一生还，而燧皇也受了重创。这场交易便不了了之。人们也逐渐遗忘了这个故事。但岁阳却还记得。

**Jing Yuan:** They hold claim to this debt for all time. The great battle between General Teng Xiao and Ignamar was fought largely for this reason.

> **景元：**它们索偿此债，永世不忘。腾骁将军与燎原的大战，多半也是为此而起。

- ▸ You're not going back to the Seat of Divine Foresight?

**Jing Yuan:** Aiding the Ten-Lords Commission in subduing the heliobi is a legitimate reason to take a leave of absence.

> **景元：**「协助十王司镇伏岁阳。」这可是个特别正当的理由。

**Jing Yuan:** I shall treat it as a vacation and enjoy the scenery of Fyxestroll Garden — though I must admit, the wind here does chill the bones.

> **景元：**就当我给自己放个假吧，好好欣赏一下绥园，虽说这里的风真是透体寒凉啊。

> **年轻的医助：**哎哟，这边这位开拓者我认得，是星穹列车的人。我印象里听龙女大人提过他/她的。

> **老成的医助：**哎呀，你们肯帮忙，那是大大的好事。但我们俩其实还没搞清状况……

> **年轻的医助：**是啊，我们本来已经抓到了龙女大人，嘿，没想到她砰地不见了。

> **藿藿：**岁阳擅长顺着人的弱点趁虚而入…无论是谁都有弱点，这不是符玄大人的错。

**Sushang:** That's right — you were just a victim of the heliobus's trickery, Diviner Fu — no need to blame yourself.

> **素裳：**是啊，符玄大人只是受害者，千万不必自责了。

**Fu Xuan:** I must express my sincere gratitude to all of you for your help.

> **符玄：**衷心感谢各位的帮助。

**Fu Xuan:** Apologies, but the Divination Commission is in disarray at the moment. I must return and set things in order — I'm afraid I cannot see you off in person.

> **符玄：**也许有些不礼貌，但太卜司横遭磨难，本座还要回去主持大局，恕不远送。

**Guinaifen:** Wait! There's something we need to ask you.

> **桂乃芬：**啊！稍等一下，还有一件事。

**Fu Xuan:** What is it?

> **符玄：**什么事？

**You tell Fu Xuan the details of your operation:**

**Fu Xuan:** Mm, I agree that "Master Diviner Gets Possessed" wouldn't make for a good headline...

> **符玄：**「太卜被岁阳附体」这件事传出去不好听，我明白了。

**Fu Xuan:** I don't care how you spin the story, so long as you leave my name and identity out of it.

> **符玄：**本座倒是不在意你们怎么编派，只要隐去姓名和身份就行了。

### Talk to Guinaifen and post on Ghostly Grove about the anomaly having been resolved.

> *After successfully exorcising the heliobus in Fu Xuan, your next task is to publicize this victory on Ghostly Grove. However, you must minimize the public's perception of how dangerous the incident was to prevent panic, while also trying to stand out online to showcase the ghost-hunting squad's triumph. The task of writing a social media post is not any less challenging than exorcising the heliobus!*

*(Talk to Guinaifen)*

*(Opens A Foxian Tale of the Haunted: Ghostly Grove)*

- ▸ BREAKING NEWS

SHOCKING! Matrix of Prescience Is Self-Aware! Second Aurumaton Uprising IMMINENT!

- ▸ Mythopedia

Major Update of Divination Algorithm Causes Massive Workload!

- ▸ Hottest Scoops

LIVE: Construction Blunder Causes Total Divination Shutdown!

**Fu Xuan:** I have many things to attend to — please allow me to save my thanks for another day.

> **符玄：**本座还有很多工作要处理，今天的恩情，就请容许本座留到他日再答谢吧。

**Huohuo:** We won't delay you any longer. We need to go to Fyxestroll Garden and seal Keralum away.

> **藿藿：**那就不打扰了，接下来咱们也该回绥园把犀焰封禁起来了。

### Cast the heliobus into the suppression tower

> *The only good heliobus is a captured heliobus. The only place they should go is the Suppression Towers in Fyxestroll Garden.*

*(Interact with the Suppression Tower, Swallowsong Pavilion)*

Expel evil and capture stray spirits... With the support of the Ten Lords Commission's spiritfarers, you seal the heliobi within the Lunaumbra Gourd.

**Jing Yuan:** It seems that your operations have met with some success.

> **景元：**看来你们的行动还挺顺利的。

**Jing Yuan:** I've been browsing through some of the stories on Ghostly Grove myself — they make for good reading.

> **景元：**我也在浏览「罗浮杂俎」上的种种奇闻轶事，有趣的很。

- ▸ Did you hear about the Master Diviner?

> ▸ 这么说你知道太卜的事？

**Jing Yuan:** As the saying goes, serious divination concerns serious matters. It is inevitable that someone performing divination day in and day out for the Xianzhou would acquire inner demons, and heliobus can take advantage of those. Fortunately, with your help, Fu Xuan prevailed over her intruder.

> **景元：**所谓善易者不卜。常年为仙舟卜算吉凶，难免会产生诸多心魔，被岁阳趁虚而入。好在有你们的帮助，符卿已安然度过此劫。

- ▸ What do you think about destiny?

> ▸ 你怎么看待命运？

**Jing Yuan:** Do you enjoy listening to storytellers and watching immersia?

> **景元：**你喜欢听人说书，看幻戏吗？

**Jing Yuan:** In both cases, tales have a predetermined ending — but you shouldn't let that stop you from enjoying them, should you?

> **景元：**说书也好，幻戏也罢，所有故事的结局都是注定的，你该不会因此而放弃享受这些故事吧？

**Jing Yuan:** The same is true of life. Whether the end is predestined or not, it's worth living.

> **景元：**人生和命运也是同样的道理。无论结局是否注定，都值得一过。

- ▸ I'm heading off.

> ▸ 先走了。

**Jing Yuan:** Very well, then. Miss Hanya is waiting for you by the suppression towers.

> **景元：**不打扰了，寒鸦小姐正在镇妖塔等着你们呢。

### Talk to Hanya about how things have been recently.

> *Madam Hanya single-handedly established the ghost-hunting squad. Report to her about the situation in the Divination Commission.*

*(Interact with the Suppression Tower, Swallowsong Pavilion again, optional)*

*(Shows one of the following dialogues)*

From an outworlder's perspective, it almost seems like the matrix seals on Xianzhou are all from the Divination Commission, but that's not true. Only 70% of the seals come from the Divination Commission, 20% from the Ten-Lords Commission, and the rest are random unintelligible symbols.

You're not sure if the Evil Binding Matrix is still functioning, but the moment you close your eyes, you imagine your body inflates to the size of a hill. How scary would it be to pluck out the pillar in the middle of the matrix with your bare hands?

According to the spiritfarers of the Ten-Lords Commission, there was a heliobus who reincarnated as a Vidyadhara and entered the human world to repay a benefactor for saving its life. However, once it revealed its true form, a Ten-Lords Commission spiritfarer captured and sealed it in a suppression tower, which was later destroyed.

*(Talk to Hanya)*

**Hanya:** On behalf of the Ten-Lords Commission, I commend you for your great service in resolving the Divination Commission crisis. I will honor all of you before the Ten Lords.

> **寒鸦：**各位代表十王司弭平了太卜司中的灾异，功勋彪炳，令小女子也与有荣焉。我会上秉十王，表彰诸位。

**Huohuo:** Thanks, Madam Hanya...

> **藿藿：**谢谢寒鸦大人的赞美。

**Huohuo:** ...but we really couldn't have defeated Keralum without Miss Qingque's help.

> **藿藿：**这一次全都是靠了青雀小姐的帮助，咱们才能顺利收伏犀焰。

**Hanya:** I didn't realize that one as skilled as Diviner Fu would also be susceptible to the power of the heliobi.

> **寒鸦：**没想到如符玄大人这般神机妙算的人杰，也会受岁阳蛊惑。

**Hanya:** That said... I can understand her plight. After all, we are all moving forward on our own unseen paths... in no uncertain terms.

> **寒鸦：**话说回来…我也能理解她的苦恼。毕竟我们都是在各自看不见的道路上分毫不差地前行着。

**Hanya:** Judges are instructed by the Ten Lords to carry out their orders, but no one ever explains their meaning to us...

> **寒鸦：**判官奉行十王诰谕，执行命令，却从没有人向我们解释其含义……

**Hanya:** Just like the master diviner — no matter what cruel and horrible paths she foresaw, as long as it benefited the Luofu, she had to make a choice.

> **寒鸦：**就像太卜大人无论看到何等残酷的、可怕的选择，只要有利于罗浮众生，她都要选择。

**Hanya:** Those choices may be correct and far-sighted, but we are, after all, flesh and blood, and we cannot help but agonize over the process of making them.

> **寒鸦：**也许最终这些决策都是正确的，高瞻远瞩的，但我们毕竟也是血肉凡胎，还是会为做出选择的过程不由得感到痛苦。

**Guinaifen:** Madam Hanya...

> **桂乃芬：**寒鸦大人……

**Hanya:** I apologize, I was getting carried away. I think all the paperwork must have worn down my mental acuity. Today's business is finished. Please get some good rest!

> **寒鸦：**抱歉，小女子走神了，想必是案牍工作有损心识。今日之事已经完毕，还请各位好好休息吧！

**Guinaifen:** Right! Let's call it a day. And you too, Judge Hanya — don't work yourself to death.

> **桂乃芬：**对！今天就先解散吧，判官大人也是，可别把自己给累坏了。

**Sushang:** It's finally time to get back to bed. Goodbye, everyone.

> **素裳：**终于可以又躺回床上了。各位告辞啦。

- ▸ I'm totally beat, too. Chat more in the group later.
- ▸ Remember to share the account updates.

【游戏内短信 · Why don't we start with Huohuo and talk about what we did today?】

**Sushang:** Why don't we start with Huohuo and talk about what we did today?

> **素裳：**要不藿藿先讲讲咱们今天的收获吧？

**Huohuo:** Today we sealed the heliobus Keralum. Thanks for all the help, everybody.

> **藿藿：**今天我们封禁了岁阳犀焰，这都是要感谢各位的协助

**Huohuo:** By the way, Miss Qingque looked so cool~

> **藿藿：**顺带提一句，青雀小姐好帅～

**Guinaifen:** True dat

> **桂乃芬：**确实

**Sushang:** Yeah

> **素裳：**确实

**Guinaifen:** Let's not forget that we're internet famous now! We've dealt with all sorts of things.
