## 009　Fyxestroll Gathering (III)　绥园聚首•其三

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Fyxestroll Garden　|　角色：Guinaifen; Huohuo; Sushang; Trailblazer

### Meet up with your teammates in Fyxestroll Garden.

> *Once again, you reunite with your fellow ghost hunters at Fyxestroll Garden. Scanning through the posts on Ghostly Grove and conducting investigations have become routine tasks for you.*

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Guinaifen:** I don't think searching like this is the best way...

> **桂乃芬：**我觉得这么找没什么效率……

**Huohuo:** "Diner haunted! Evil spirit breaks into restaurant and eats double its body weight in food!" ...Do you think Tail did this? Seems like his style...

> **藿藿：**「食客恶鬼缠身闯进餐厅暴饮暴食吃下体重一倍的食物！」…有没有可能是尾巴干的？像它的作风……

**Sushang:** Sounds like a very hungry criminal came up with a very poor excuse...

> **素裳：**这应该单纯是吃霸王餐被抓以后在给自己找借口。

**Huohuo:** What about this one... "Paranormal activity reported at exclusive horror immersia! Guests experience haunting within seven days of screening!"

> **藿藿：**那这个呢…「限制级恐怖幻戏发生灵异事件！七天内多位租借人厄运缠身！」。

**Huohuo:** It reminds me of when I used to watch horror immersia to train my courage... whenever there was a suspenseful moment, Tail would jump up and scare me.

> **藿藿：**这让我想起以前看恐怖幻戏练胆时，尾巴总喜欢在最紧要的地方突然冒出来吓唬我。

**Sushang:** Guys, I feel like this is just some sort of prank... or a marketing stunt to boost ticket sales.

> **素裳：**家人啊，我觉着这应该只是某种恶作剧…也不排除是有人想拿这个当噱头，提升幻戏的销量。

- ▸ What are you guys up to?

> ▸ 这是在干什么？

- ▸ Have we planned out our next steps yet?

> ▸ 在挑选今天的行动吗？

**Sushang:** Huohuo has been browsing Ghostly Grove for hours. Every time she finds a suspicious incident, she says: "Do you think Tail did this?"

> **素裳：**藿藿刷了几个时辰的「罗浮杂俎」，每逮着一个可疑事件就问咱们「你说这是不是尾巴干的？」

**Sushang:** The problem is, any suspicious incident could be Tail-related when you think about it...

> **素裳：**尾巴真是日理万机，啥坏事都跟它搭上边了。

**Huohuo:** I feel like Tail is still around... but if so, why didn't he come back to see me?

> **藿藿：**我有种直觉，尾巴还在。可它要是真的还在，为什么不回来见见我呢？

- ▸ He'll come back.

> ▸ 它会回来的。

**Sushang:** He was your tail for so many years, I'm sure he'd come back to say hi... or bye.

> **素裳：**我也觉得，毕竟被绑在一起这么多年，多多少少得念个旧。就算离开也要回来告别一下嘛。

- ▸ Well, he is a heliobus.

> ▸ 它毕竟是岁阳…

**Guinaifen:** That's right — we can't really know what a heliobus is thinking, right? Tail was sealed away for ages and then suddenly let free... If it were me, I'd want some away time.

> **桂乃芬：**是啊，妖怪的心思你别猜。尾巴被封印了这许多年，大概就像我一样，突然得到了自由，铁定会有一段时间到处乱跑的。

**Guinaifen:** You'll just have to be a little patient.

> **桂乃芬：**当大哥大姐的，还得多多包容，耐心等待呀。

**Guinaifen:** Anyway, even if you don't find Tail, you're still a great judge — the success of our ghost-hunting squad proves it.

> **桂乃芬：**不过，就算失去了尾巴，藿藿一样是个厉害的判官呀，咱们这么多次行动的成功不就证明了这一点。

**Huohuo:** I... Thank you, Miss Gui. I... I don't really get compliments. Without the encouragement of you squad members...

> **藿藿：**唔，谢谢你，桂小姐。你这样夸我，我、我真的很不好意思。如果没有你们的帮助鼓励……

**Huohuo:** Ahem. Working with you guys has made me feel a little more confident... but it's hard not to think about the days when Tail was still around...

> **藿藿：**虽然这些日子和你们一起行动，让我渐渐变得更有自信了一些。但也总是不自觉地想起尾巴还在的日子……

**Huohuo:** Back then I was a useless coward — I'd hide at the first sign of trouble. Tail would always complain about it... but he'd also get me out of danger.

> **藿藿：**那时候我只是个没用的胆小鬼，一有风吹草动就要躲起来。尾巴总是一边嘴臭我，一边帮我解决麻烦……

**Guinaifen:** Reminds me of me and my brother — sort of a love-hate relationship.

> **桂乃芬：**有点我哥那意思了，唉呀，真是孽缘呐。

**Huohuo:** Hey! Look at this!

> **藿藿：**啊！你们快来看！

**Sushang:** Did you find Tail?

> **素裳：**找到尾巴了？

### Talk to Huohuo and check the post asking for help.

> *Having completed your adventure, you reunite with your fellow ghost hunters at Fyxestroll Garden. Scanning through the posts on Ghostly Grove and conducting investigations have become routine tasks for you.*

*(Talk to Huohuo)*

**Huohuo:** Hey! Look at this!

> **藿藿：**啊！你们快来看！

*(Opens A Foxian Tale of the Haunted: Ghostly Grove)*

OMG! Cloud Knight caught doing the unthinkable at Alchemy Commission!

(Accept Investigation Assignment)

**Guinaifen:** Is this... something Tail would do?

> **桂乃芬：**这…会是尾巴闹出来的事吗？

**Huohuo:** Tail or no Tail — it's definitely the work of a heliobus!

> **藿藿：**尾巴的话题先放在一边…这这这肯定是岁阳作乱啊！

**Sushang:** Let's hurry up and get to the scene — there'll be trouble to pay if we're too late.

> **素裳：**咱们抓紧出发吧，晚了可就麻烦了。

- ▸ Let's go.

> ▸ 出发吧。

- ▸ Shouldn't we talk to Cirrus first?

> ▸ 不先去问问浮烟？

**Guinaifen:** We don't have time for that. Even if Cirrus does know something, by the time we've finished asking questions, it'll be too late!

> **桂乃芬：**没这个时间了家人。浮烟也许知道些什么，但等咱们问完话过去，黄花菜都凉了！

**Huohuo:** Be careful everyone — this sounds like a very aggressive heliobus.

## 010　Sword Essence　剑精

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Alchemy Commission (Location)　|　角色：Guinaifen; Huohuo; Sushang; Trailblazer; Yanqing; Jing Yuan; Cirrus; Apyra　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''Fyxestroll Gathering (III)'' completed

### Head to the Alchemy Commission and locate the delirious Cloud Knight.

> *A Cloud Knight out of control... if it isn't a case of mara-struck, then it would be the work of a heliobus. Following the description in the post, you make your way to the Alchemy Commission to investigate.*

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Vigilant Cloud Knight:** Hey, calm down!

> **时刻戒备的云骑：**兄弟，冷静啊！

**Delirious Cloud Knight:** I just learned a new technique — I'll use it to end your life!

> **神智昏乱的云骑：**我刚刚领悟了一招！便用这招来了结你的性命！

- ▸ What's with this guy?

> ▸ 他这是怎么了？

**Vigilant Cloud Knight:** Unauthorized personnel should keep their distance — it's dangerous here!

> **时刻戒备的云骑：**无关人员切勿靠近，这里很危险！

**Sushang:** Actually, we're... not really unauthorized, are we, Judge?

> **素裳：**其实咱们…也不算无关人员对吧，判官大人？

**Vigilant Cloud Knight:** Is that... Sushang? And a judge... Why are the Ten-Lords Commission here?

> **时刻戒备的云骑：**你是…素裳？判官…你怎么和十王司的人走在一起了？

**Sushang:** It's a very long story, so let's deal with the problem at hand first.

> **素裳：**解释起来实在费时费力，咱们先处理眼前的问题吧。

**Huohuo:** Mr. Cloud Knight sir, the Ten-Lords Commission will handle this. Thank you for your cooperation.

> **藿藿：**云骑大哥，眼下这儿由十王司的人接管了，请你配合。

**Vigilant Cloud Knight:** Understood... Please, take mercy on him.

> **时刻戒备的云骑：**我明白了，也请判官大人手下留情。

**Huohuo:** Don't worry, this sort of thing is our specialty.

> **藿藿：**我们会有分寸的。

*(Begin battle against Cloud Knights Patroller ×3, Wraith Warden ×1)*

**Delirious Cloud Knight:** See how you like this!

> **神智昏乱的云骑：**那看看这一招如何！

*(After the battle)*

**After a struggle, you subdue the crazed Cloud Knight.:**

**Vigilant Cloud Knight:** Judge Huohuo, is he... alright?

> **时刻戒备的云骑：**判官大人，他…没事吧？

**Huohuo:** His life isn't in danger, but his hands and feet are spasming... and his speech is incoherent. I think he's experiencing a delusion that he can't snap out of.

> **藿藿：**倒是不危及性命，只是他手足痉挛，语无伦次，似乎正处于某种无法摆脱的妄想中。

**Delirious Cloud Knight:** I feel... like I've been drained of my strength...

> **神智昏乱的云骑：**我感觉…像是被抽干了力气……

**Huohuo:** Mr. Cloud Knight sir, are you okay? Can you answer a few questions for me? Do you remember what happened to you?

> **藿藿：**这位云骑大哥，你还好吗？能回答我几个问题吗？还记得自己身上到底发生了什么事情吗？

**Delirious Cloud Knight:** I... I think so... Let me think for a moment...

> **神智昏乱的云骑：**我隐约记得一些…让我回忆回忆……

**Delirious Cloud Knight:** I received a report today about an infestation of abominations here at the Alchemy Commission — the captain sent me to clear it out. What happened after that was like a dream... I can't explain it.

> **神智昏乱的云骑：**今天接报，说丹鼎司这儿有孽物出没，队长派我来清剿。不过，之后的事情…简直、简直就像是做了个梦，太奇怪了。

**Delirious Cloud Knight:** My weapon suddenly started talking to me... it taught me fighting techniques — which moves to avoid, which moves to use, which stances to adopt to retain balance... then it told me to relax and let it take control against the enemy.

> **神智昏乱的云骑：**我的武器突然对我开口说话了…它指点我武艺，说我这一招徒有其表，那一式走了力气，它让我放松身体，由它来帮我舞刀杀敌。

**Sushang:** That sounds just like... a heliobus!

> **素裳：**怎么跟我娘一样…不是，我是说，这件事果然和岁阳有关！

**Delirious Cloud Knight:** I followed its instructions... To my surprise, it felt like my combat skills had greatly improved. Still, it told me I was incompetent, like a piece of rotten wood. It wanted to find a new apprentice to carry forward the art.

> **神智昏乱的云骑：**我按它的指示，由它指挥，竟然觉得自己武艺大有长进。可它却说，我实在是资质驽钝，朽木一块。他要另收一名弟子，将自己的技艺发扬光大。

### Seek Jing Yuan's assistance.

> **桂乃芬：**对了，有没有可能，今天藿藿突然得到了关于尾巴的线索…这样一来，以她的性格一定会去找回尾巴的。

**Hanya:** ...And yet she didn't come to any of us for help. That's not like her.

> **寒鸦：**但是藿藿既没有找你们，也没有来找我。这很反常。

**Hanya:** Given the type of person she is, if she did have any clues, she would seek out our help. If she hasn't shared anything with us, it means there is only one possibility.

> **寒鸦：**以藿藿的性格，假设她得到了「线索」，她多半会向外求援。但…如果她没有向我们任何人提及此事，唯有一种可能。

- ▸ She's been deceived?

> ▸ 她受人欺骗？

**Guinaifen:** Correct. And I think I might know who the culprit is.

> **桂乃芬：**嗯，我已经有了怀疑对象。

- ▸ Perhaps she doesn't trust us.

> ▸ 她不信任我们。

**Hanya:** After working together for the last few days, it is clear that Huohuo has already formed a close bond with each of you. It is hard for me to imagine she would simply wander off on her own... Unless someone deceived her...

> **寒鸦：**我想经历这几日的行动，各位应该很明白，藿藿早已同你们合作无间了。我很难想像她会独自一人行动。除非有人蒙骗了她……

- ▸ She must have some reason for acting alone...

> ▸ 她有不得已独自行动的原因。

**Sushang:** Right, maybe someone deceived her with news about Tail — told her "she could only go alone." Sounds like a trick some nasty kidnapper would use.

> **素裳：**嗯，也许有人用尾巴的消息蒙骗了她，并且告诉她「只能一个人去」。嗯嗯，很像是某些绑票的坏家伙会用的招数。

**Hanya:** Cirrus... The only one who may have knowledge of Tail's whereabouts is Cirrus... whose plans were foiled due to Tail's actions.

> **寒鸦：**浮烟…唯一能知晓尾巴下落的，只有因为它的融入而功败垂成的浮烟。

**Guinaifen:** Even if Cirrus doesn't know where Tail is, given the shared history, they wouldn't hesitate to cause trouble...

> **桂乃芬：**就算浮烟不知道，要说这里谁最打算使坏，也只有可能是那家伙了。

**Hanya:** Let's go, we must try to squeeze some answers out of Cirrus.

> **寒鸦：**走吧，审问浮烟，务必要从它嘴里撬出些什么。

## 012　Foxian Dream　狐梦

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Fyxestroll Garden　|　角色：Guinaifen; Huohuo; Sushang; Trailblazer; Hanya; Cirrus; Tail; Jing Yuan　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''Fyxestroll Gathering (IV)'' completed

### Get information out of Cirrus.

> *Faced with the sudden incident, Madam Hanya goes through Huohuo's potential whereabouts with your group. You discover the seemingly "docile" Cirrus may hold a vital clue about Tail and has used it as bait to ensnare Huohuo. While you are not entirely certain, confronting the cunning heliobus seems to be the best course of action.*

*(Approach Cirrus)*

**Cirrus:** Well, well, insects who don't know when to quit. I sense you have questions... Go on, ask away.

> **浮烟：**瞧瞧，几只虫子气势汹汹冲我而来，嗡嗡嗡。想问什么就问吧。

**Guinaifen:** Huohuo is missing, and I'm willing to bet you know something about it!

> **桂乃芬：**藿藿不见了，你这坏东西一定知道点什么，对吧！

**Cirrus:** How careless... Your companion vanishes right under your noses, and you fail to notice. Why am I the one you're pestering?

> **浮烟：**你们离心离德，连自己的同伴离开都毫不知情，为什么要来找我的麻烦——

- ▸ She left without a word — someone clearly deceived her.
- ▸ You lied to her about Tail's whereabouts.

**Hanya:** The Ten-Lords Commission might not be able to completely eradicate the heliobi, but we have plenty of ways of dealing with them...

> **寒鸦：**十王司虽然无法彻底消灭岁阳，但我们有的是对付岁阳的手段……

**Cirrus:** Ooh, how threatening, hehe... Save your breath, I know your tactics.

> **浮烟：**你们的手段我都见识过啦，嘻嘻，像我这样劫持人质性命的坏东西，可经受不住您的恐吓。

**Cirrus:** Let's cut to the chase. I do know something about it.

> **浮烟：**不妨把话说开了吧，这件事确实与我有关。

**Huohuo:** According to the Ten-Lords Commission's understanding of heliobi behavior, they tend to roam densely populated areas in search of hosts... and wherever they go, there are bound to be reports of "supernatural phenomena."

**Sushang:** I knew it!

> **素裳：**果然！

**Cirrus:** Believe it or not, while you were distracted, she came and found me. She asked me if I knew where "Mr. Tail" was, and I simply gave her a clue...

> **浮烟：**在你们不知道的时候，有一天那个姑娘来问我「知不知道尾巴到哪里去了」，我向她透露了一点线索，她的小脑瓜自然会去拼凑答案。

**Cirrus:** She searched high and low, but came back empty-handed, of course. If only you could have seen her expression— simultaneously terrified and desperate to know more — utterly hilarious!

> **浮烟：**当然了，凭她怎么找也一无所获。于是她又来求了我好几遍，你们没看到她那又怕又要问的表情，笑死！

**Cirrus:** In the end, I became fed up with her constant nagging. I told her that her beloved Tail had splintered and been reduced to an ember without a single memory... You should've seen her face!

> **浮烟：**后来我嫌她烦，我又说「尾巴跟我一样四分五裂了，变成了什么都记不得的小火苗」，你们没看到她脸色吓得煞白，哈哈哈哈！

- ▸ Don't bully Huohuo!

**Cirrus:** Aw, does it hurt? You'll be pleased to know I didn't drag out the suffering. I told her that her Tail was hiding in Cloudford — preparing to flee the Xianzhou on a starskiff.

> **浮烟：**心疼啦？我可没有折磨她太久，我告诉她「尾巴现在躲在流云渡，正准备随星槎逃出仙舟」。

- ▸ ...Where is she!?

**Cirrus:** I started feeling sorry for her. Out of pity I told her that her Tail was hiding in Cloudford — preparing to flee the Xianzhou on a starskiff.

> **浮烟：**我念她心诚，大发慈悲告诉她「尾巴现在躲在流云渡，正准备随星槎逃出仙舟」。

**Cirrus:** I also told her that the Ten-Lords Commission was in the process of recapturing all the heliobi. If she were to turn to you for help, "Mr. Tail" would be imprisoned — even if she managed to find him. Looks like she took the bait...

> **浮烟：**我还告诉她，如今十王司正在回收岁阳。如果她求助于你们，就算见到尾巴，它也会被镇入牢狱。显然，她信了……

**Hanya:** Lies! You can't possibly know where Tail disappeared to!

> **寒鸦：**你在说谎，你不可能知道尾巴究竟到哪里去了？

**Cirrus:** Maybe I do, maybe I don't. But I have no doubt about one thing: my friends are ready and waiting for her in Cloudford!

> **浮烟：**知不知道两说，但在流云渡那儿却有我的同族等着她，嘻嘻嘻！

- ▸ How could Huohuo be tricked so easily?

**Cirrus:** I tricked you, didn't I?

> **浮烟：**你不也被我骗过吗？哈哈哈哈。

- ▸ Then that means Huohuo is...

**Cirrus:** You should have faith in your friend... Perhaps Huohuo is safe and sound — perhaps she's managed to subdue the heliobi? If you rush to Cloudford, there's still a chance you might find her...

> **浮烟：**你们要对朋友有信心啊，也许藿藿安然无恙，也许她将那只岁阳顺手降服了呢？也许你们现在赶去流云渡，还能找到她？

**Guinaifen:** Not good! Sushang, (Trailblazer) let's head to Cloudford, quickly!

> **桂乃芬：**混蛋！素裳，开拓者，咱们赶紧到流云渡去找藿藿！

**Hanya:** The paperwork will have to wait. I'm coming with you.

> **寒鸦：**看来案牍工作得搁下了，我和你们一起出发。

*(Talk to Cirrus, optional)*

**Cirrus:** Still here, huh? Then I guess you're not too concerned about your friend.

> **浮烟：**还在这儿转悠吗？你对朋友还真不上心哪。

### Head to Cloudford and find Huohuo

> *With surprising candor, Cirrus confesses to its deeds and reveals crucial information about Huohuo's whereabouts, even sparing you from a fight. Although this raises suspicions, the top priority remains rescuing Huohuo.*

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Guinaifen:** Huohuo! Huohuo!

> **桂乃芬：**藿藿！藿藿！

**Sushang:** What do we do now? I'll go get help from the Alchemy Commission.

> **素裳：**这下可怎么办？我这就去把丹鼎司的人叫来。

**Hanya:** Hold on, let me take a look at her.

> **寒鸦：**镇定，让小女子来瞧瞧。

**Hanya does a check on Huohuo:**

**Hanya:** She's unresponsive. It looks like a heliobus has taken control of her mind.

> **寒鸦：**毫无反应，应是被岁阳劫持了心识。

- ▸ How could this happen?

**Hanya:** Huohuo spent a long time in coexistence with a heliobus — it is likely she became accustomed to that state. With Tail now gone, she was relatively defenseless against other heliobi.

> **寒鸦：**藿藿长久与岁阳共存，她恐怕早已习惯了这样的状态。尾巴离去后，她的身体对其他岁阳来说，也是处于完全不设防的状态。

- ▸ Should we try using the Harmonic Chime?

**Hanya:** This isn't just a heliobus illusion, it's a complete sensory barrier — cutting off the individual from the outside world. In this case, even the Harmonic Chime won't work.

> **寒鸦：**这并非岁阳编织的幻境迷障，而是封闭了五感，将人类彻底与外界的信息隔绝。这样一来，连能融入幻境的火铃也毫无用武之地了。

**Hanya:** This isn't something the healers at the Alchemy Commission can handle. I'll take Huohuo back to the Ten-Lords Commission — perhaps they'll have an answer.

> **寒鸦：**这不是丹鼎司医士能处理的情况，我会将藿藿带回十王司，再看看有什么手段。

**Hanya:** As for now, I'm temporarily suspending the activities of the ghost-hunting squad.

> **寒鸦：**另外，捉鬼小队的任务眼下必须暂时中止了。

**Guinaifen:** But...

> **桂乃芬：**可是……

**Hanya:** The situation has changed completely. Without the assistance of a judge, continuing would place all of you in grave danger — defeating the purpose of the squad.

> **寒鸦：**如今情况已经截然不同，没有判官相助，会让各位深陷危险境地，这与成立小队的初衷不符。

**Guinaifen:** But, Miss Hanya, this is to save our friend!

> **桂乃芬：**可是，寒鸦小姐，这一回的任务是救回我们的朋友啊！

**Hanya:** I know, I understand. But please, let the Ten-Lords Commission deal with this. We'll find a way to extract this heliobus from Huohuo.

> **寒鸦：**各位急切的心思我明白，请交给十王司吧，我们自会有法子将这岁阳揪出来。

**Guinaifen:** Miss Hanya, tell me straight... Does the Ten-Lords Commission actually have a solution?

> **桂乃芬：**寒鸦小姐，请诚实地告诉我，十王司有什么办法吗？

**Hanya:** ...

> **寒鸦：**……

- ▸ Surely the Ten-Lords can fix this?
- ▸ Let's interrogate Cirrus!

**Guinaifen:** Yeah, Cirrus is bound to know what this heliobus is up to.

> **桂乃芬：**对啊，浮烟肯定知道岁阳到底能做什么。

**Sushang:** No, we shouldn't do what they expect us to. They've been manipulating us from Fyxestroll Garden all along. We need to find out what they really want before we go any further.

> **素裳：**不行，它坐在绥园中，却将我们一步步引导到如此境地。在没有搞明白它的意图之前，我们不能再听之任之了。

**Hanya:** We are not completely out of options.

> **寒鸦：**办法并不是没有。

**Hanya:** It might be possible to use another heliobus to drive out the one that seized Huohuo's mind.

> **寒鸦：**让另一只岁阳帮助我们除魔，驱逐占夺了藿藿心智的家伙。

**Guinaifen:** But why would a heliobus ever want to help people? Wait... Miss Hanya, do you mean to say that...

> **桂乃芬：**可是怎么可能有岁阳愿意帮助人类，等等——寒鸦小姐的意思是？

Hanya produces the Lunaumbra Gourd from her sleeves.

**Hanya:** I've been keeping Tail here, all along...

> **寒鸦：**尾巴…其实被我收在此处。

**Guinaifen:** W-What?

> **桂乃芬：**什…什么？

**Tail:** Don't look so surprised, young lady!

> **尾巴：**小姑娘，别一副大惊小怪的样子！

**Sushang:** Surprised!? Huohuo has been worried sick trying to find you, and now something else has gone after her!

> **素裳：**大惊小怪？！藿藿可是为了找你茶不思饭不想的，现在还被另一个坏东西乘虚而入了！

**Guinaifen:** You flaming furball! What the heck is going on!? Did you know about this the whole time, Miss Hanya?

> **桂乃芬：**你这个火焰团子！到底是怎么回事？难不成寒鸦小姐，你一直都知道吗？

**Hanya:** As per our rules, any free-roaming heliobus must be sealed away. I was merely following protocol. Besides, it was Tail who requested this...

> **寒鸦：**原则上，游离在外的岁阳必须被封印，我只是按规制行事。而且，是尾巴拜托我这么做的……

**Tail:** It's a long story...

> **尾巴：**说来话长……

**Tail:** I only recall a moment during that fateful night when my consciousness was on the verge of shattering into a million pieces... time stood still.

> **尾巴：**本大爷只记得那天夜里意识快要四分五裂的那一瞬，时间就像是停止了一样。

**Tail:** I watched myself parting from Ignamar — drifting without purpose... Then, before I knew what had happened, the girl had retrieved me, and a judge of the Ten-Lords Commission had sealed me within a tail.

> **尾巴：**然后老子看到了自己从燎原身上分出来、漫无目的地流浪…看到自己被这小姑娘捡到，然后，一眨眼老子被十王司判官封印在尾巴上……

**Tail:** I believe you humans call this kind of thing an "end-of-life-experience..." but why would a heliobus experience one of those? Ridiculous...

> **尾巴：**你们人类管这种事叫「回光返照」对吧，为什么岁阳也会有这样的体验，真是见了鬼了…

**Tail:** Since I'd slipped the leash, I thought I might as well change bodies and savor a bit of freedom. But then—

> **尾巴：**老子本想，既然都逃出来了，也该换个身体好好逍遥自在一下了。但是——

**Hanya:** Lucky for you, you met me.

> **寒鸦：**它运气好，遇到了我。

**Tail:** ...My luck took a sour turn when I ran into this demoness... I mean, judge.

> **尾巴：**倒了本大爷的血霉，遇见了这个女鬼…我是说判官。

**Tail:** When I was with Huohuo, she'd constantly rant about me — whining about all the problems I'd caused her, how I'd ruined her life.

> **尾巴：**本大爷在的时候，藿藿老是嫌弃老子，动不动唠唠叨叨数落我给她带来了多少麻烦，她有多讨厌现在的生活。

**Tail:** Hehe, it was only after I vanished that she realized she'd taken my power for granted. I wanted to hear her cry about it, so in a fit of anger I had this demon— uh, judge imprison me in the gourd.

> **尾巴：**嘿嘿，不珍惜本大爷这样的强力上级，等到失去时哭天抢地后悔莫及。老子只想听她哭。所以我一气之下，就让这女鬼…判官把老子给放进葫芦里。

**Hanya:** You had had your wish. Now, I'm offering you a chance to regain your freedom... not by letting you go, but by going back to the way things were. Do we have a deal?

> **寒鸦：**你如愿以偿了，现在，我给你一个重获自由的机会。当然，不是放任你离去，而是让一切恢复原样。成交？

**Tail:** ...

> **尾巴：**……

**Hanya:** Can I trust you, Tail?

> **寒鸦：**尾巴，我可以相信你吗？

**Tail:** Of course! We're pretty much co-workers at this point!

> **尾巴：**我说，咱们也算是老同事了，放心交给本大爷吧！

**Tail:** Hmm... So all I have to do is possess Huohuo and take out those other pesky heliobi... right?

> **尾巴：**所以，只要让本大爷附身藿藿，把另一个碍事的家伙给吃了，就没问题了吧？

### Enter Huohuo's hallucination with Tail.

> *The heliobus has sealed Huohuo's five senses, rendering the "Harmonic Chime" ineffective. Thankfully, Tail appears at this time. "Using a heliobus to exorcise another heliobus" sure is a new way of exorcism. It is time to let Mr. Tail break into the illusion.*

*(Talk to Tail)*

**Tail:** Time to give this heedless creature a reality check!

> **尾巴：**得给这不长眼的东西长点儿记性，让它明白自己惹祸上身了！

- ▸ It's all up to you now, Tail!

**Tail:** It's exorcism o'clock!

(Enter Exploratory Excursion Illusory Maze: Immola)

- ▸ Wait a minute, I'm still not ready.

**Tail:** Not ready? I'm the one doing the possessing here!

> **尾巴：**本大爷去附身，你没准备好关老子什么事？

**Tail:** Ugh... Fine. As a courtesy, I'll wait. Tell me when you're ready.

> **尾巴：**…哎，本大爷卖你个面子，准备好了再叫我。

### Search for traces of Tail.

> *Huohuo, beguiled by the heliobus, can only searches for traces of Tail in the illusion. She is yet unaware of what she will encounter — heliobi will find the host's most painful and vulnerable emotions, and amplify them to unbearable heights.*

**Huohuo:** Where... where am I? T—Tail, are you here?

**Huohuo:** I... I feel like I've been possessed. Tail, is that you?

**Huohuo:** ...No, it can't be him. Tail always said only the most privileged are worthy of being his host.

> **藿藿：**…别胡思乱想了，现在该怎么办？

**Huohuo:** Mom?

**Huohuo:** Is this an illusion? Mom, what are you doing here? I've missed you so much.

Huohuo's Mother: S—Stay back! Don't come any closer!

Huohuo's Mother: I don't know who you are, but you're not my Huohuo. Keep that filthy thing you're carrying away from me!

**Huohuo:** But I'm still me... I'm the one in control...

> **藿藿：**…妹妹她还好吗？

- ▸ ...Is my sister okay?

**Huohuo:** ...Is my sister okay?

Huohuo's Mother: The farther you stay away from her, the better.

**Huohuo:** ...

Even though she knows this is just a mirage made by the heliobus, Huohuo's breathing still gets heavier after her mother leaves.

**Huohuo:** ...

> **藿藿：**……

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Huohuo:** H—Hey... I remember you...

> **藿藿：**你们…我记得你们……

**Mean classmate:** Huohuo's tail is all singed! It looks like a giant sparkler!

> **恶意的同学：**藿藿的大尾巴，烧没啦！藿藿的大尾巴，像烟花！

> **害怕的同学：**怪物，离我们远一点！

> **藿藿：**……

> **恶意的同学：**你不是装可怜！听说你爸妈也不要你了，确实可怜！

- ▸ You're... You're all so mean!

**Huohuo:** You're all so mean...

> **藿藿：**你们太过分了…

**Scared Classmate:** Blame that freaky tail of yours! No one else has a tail that catches fire! Get lost!

> **害怕的同学：**要怪就怪你那奇怪的尾巴！我们都没有会燃烧的尾巴，你走开！

- ▸ ...

**Huohuo:** ...

> **藿藿：**……

**Scared Classmate:** Playing dumb? Don't wanna talk? Ugh!

> **害怕的同学：**不说话，装哑巴，呸呸呸！

**Scared Classmate:** We shouldn't be talking to a monster, anyway. Let's get out of here

> **害怕的同学：**我们不要和怪物说话了，快走快走。

Even though she knows this is just a mirage made by the heliobus, Huohuo still feels depressed after her classmates leave.

> **藿藿：**藿藿…不是怪物啊。

**Huohuo:** What should I do?

> **藿藿：**我该怎么办……

> **藿藿：**尾巴……

> **尾巴：**哎哟我去，你就说回没回来吧？！

> **藿藿：**尾巴，你不是我的幻觉啊…你是真正的尾巴！你是真的尾巴！你回来了！

**Huohuo:** Where have you been all this time? I thought you were already...

> **藿藿：**这些日子你都跑哪去了，我还以为你…以为你已经……

**Tail:** ...Dead? Well, I haven't exactly had a picnic — exploding into countless shards, being your shadow for all those rotten years — it's about time I had some good luck!

> **尾巴：**已经死了？老子过得也不轻松呀，被炸成了无数小碎片，老费力地又融在一起了…老子跟着你倒了这么多年的霉，总该得有些好运气吧！

**Tail:** I thought about getting out of here, but I couldn't shake the feeling there was something wrong... so I came back to see if you could manage on your own, or if you were still a scaredy-cat.

> **尾巴：**老子原本是打算跑路的，但是，这不是不放心你嘛，特意来确认一下，你这个怂包到底能不能行，有没有长进？

**Huohuo:** I'm sorry... I guess I still am...

> **藿藿：**不好意思，藿藿毫无长进。

**Tail:** No, you're selling yourself short! You're daring! How you had the courage to do something so dangerous amazes me! It's not bravery you lack, but sense!

> **尾巴：**不，你也太小看自己了，你那何止是长进啊，你简直是胆大包天！一个人就敢来做这么危险的事？我怀疑你不是没有勇气，你只是没脑子！

**Huohuo:** ...What other choice did I have? I suspected that Cirrus was deceiving me, but the thought of losing you forever was... unbearable.

> **藿藿：**…那、那我能怎么办嘛，我也觉得浮烟在骗我，但要是错过就找不到了怎么办？

**Tail:** Forget about all that, I'm here now. But... did everyone really leave you because of me?

> **尾巴：**这事就别提了。嘿，藿藿，真的是因为老子的关系，这些人都离开了你吗？

**Huohuo:** You know what? They left, let them be. I'm not a people person anyway. Without you, Tail, they'd have left me just the same...

> **藿藿：**…离开就离开吧。我本来也不擅长和人相处，就算没有尾巴，他们也会离开的。

- ▸ But at least you're back now, right?

**Huohuo:** ...but you came back for me... and that's all I need.

> **藿藿：**至少现在，有尾巴愿意为我回来，我很满足了。

**Tail:** Well, since you put it like that, I suppose I have nothing to feel guilty about anymore!

> **尾巴：**既然你都这么说了，本大爷可不会愧疚的哦。

**Tail:** ...Somebody needs to keep you out of trouble! Speaking of which, there's a pesky heliobus lurking right here. I'm coming for you!

> **尾巴：**哎，我还是得盯着你，免得你又惹出什么大乱子！对了，眼前还有件麻烦事——藏起来的岁阳，立刻给老子滚出来！

**Tail:** Don't even think about escaping!

> **尾巴：**别想跑！

**Guinaifen:** Huohuo, you're awake! Seems like Tail's "operation" was a success!

**Huohuo:** Huh... Madam Hanya, Miss Gui, Sushang, and Mr./Miss (Trailblazer)...

> **藿藿：**唉、唉？！寒鸦大人，桂小姐，素裳，还有…开拓者先/小姐……

**Huohuo:** Thank you, all of you. Are you the ones who brought Tail back?

> **藿藿：**谢谢你们，是你们把尾巴带回来了吗？

**Guinaifen:** Well, where would we be without our squad leader? We had to help!

> **桂乃芬：**这有什么嘛，你是我们的队长，当然是要帮你找回来的嘛！

**Tail:** Oh, so scaredy-cat is squad leader? Well, well, look at you!

> **尾巴：**哦，小怂包现在是队长啦？了不得嘛。

**Huohuo:** That's just what Miss Guinaifen calls me... hehe.

> **藿藿：**…只、只是桂乃芬小姐这样叫我而已。

**Sushang:** Well, well, Tail actually does belong to Miss Huohuo!

> **素裳：**原来，尾巴还真的是藿藿小姐的尾巴呀！

**Tail:** Excuse me?

> **尾巴：**你在说什么？

**Hanya:** Despite Cirrus's scheming and calculating, they clearly didn't expect Tail to return to Huohuo... their plan to kidnap her was an utter failure. If they saw Huohuo as she is now, it would break their will!

> **寒鸦：**浮烟千算万算，显然无法想到尾巴早已回到了藿藿身边。这下它想要劫持判官的计划算是彻底失败了。要是让藿藿出现在它面前，想必能重挫它的斗志。

**Spiritfarer Messenger:** Madam Hanya! Urgent news from Fyxestroll Garden! The subdued heliobi are stirring again!

> **冥差的讯号：**寒鸦大人！绥园急报！被镇伏的岁阳…又有异动！

**Hanya:** ...Cirrus may not know the location of Tail, but they still figured out a way to divert everyone's attention.

> **寒鸦：**……看来，浮烟虽然不清楚尾巴的所在，但它确实想到了如何转移所有人的视线。

- ▸ This was definitely Cirrus's ruse to divert our attention

**Hanya:** Cirrus may come across as hot-headed and impulsive, but under the surface they are actually full of guile...

> **寒鸦：**那家伙虽然看似好斗莽撞，但实在是奸猾……

**Hanya:** No wonder they like to use analogies such as "pawns" and "chess" to describe people. One momentary lapse of concentration, and suddenly we find ourselves backed into a corner.

> **寒鸦：**难怪它喜欢以「棋子」喻人、以「棋局」喻势…一时疏忽竟然被它逼到了角落。

- ▸ Cirrus was waiting for us to leave Fyxestroll Garden all along.

**Huohuo:** ...It's not because of me, is it?

> **藿藿：**…不会是因为我的关系吧？

**Hanya:** There's no need to blame yourself. This was Cirrus's plan all along.

> **寒鸦：**你不必自责，这些都是浮烟早就计划好的。

**Hanya:** It looks like the "endgame" that General Jing Yuan has been trying to avoid is fast approaching. Sushang, please head to the Seat of Divine Foresight and fetch the general.

> **寒鸦：**看来，景元将军一直想拖延的「终盘」要提前了。素裳，去神策府把将军请来。

**Hanya:** Let's hurry back to Fyxestroll Garden now, while there's still a chance to turn things around. It's time to teach Cirrus a lesson.

> **寒鸦：**我们现在立刻赶回绥园，趁事情还没到无可挽回的地步，陪浮烟来一局吧。

### Talk to Cirrus.

> *Cirrus, having distracted the attention of everyone, gathered its power once again while the judges were absent from Fyxestroll Garden. Evidently, its plans failed once more. Talk to Cirrus and find out what it is planning.*

*(Approach Cirrus)*

**Cirrus:** Tsk, tsk, if it isn't the judges of the Ten-Lords Commission — sluggish as ever.

> **浮烟：**啧啧，迟钝的十王司判官终于回来了？

**Cirrus:** Oh, and is that "Mr. Tail"? Back to being a guard dog I see — how delightful... This calls for a celebration!

> **浮烟：**哎哟这不是尾巴大爷吗，看到你又当回看门狗，真是可喜可贺呀。

**Cirrus:** Still, after our last encounter, I've no intention of including you in my plans. To see Ignamar's most powerful fragment now burdened with emotions pains me. It's a pity, but you've become expendable.

> **浮烟：**只可惜，经过上一次的教训，我已经彻底放弃将你纳入到我的计划之中了，就算是「燎原」最强大的碎片，获得多余的情感后，也只是个废物。

**Tail:** Who are you calling expendable!?

> **尾巴：**你说谁是废物呢？！

**Hanya:** The Ten-Lords Commission's matrix is still intact — the heliobi cannot leave Fyxestroll Garden. You may think you have the upper hand, but you're nothing more than a caged animal. You will never leave here.

> **寒鸦：**十王司的阵法尚在，岁阳是无法离开绥园的，你自以为得计的一手不过是困兽之斗。

**Cirrus:** Leave? That was never my plan. I came to Fyxestroll Garden for one reason, and one reason alone — to fight the Luofu's formidable general.

> **浮烟：**我从未想过离开。我从洪炉中苏醒，来到绥园只为一件事情——与你罗浮的将军一战。

**Cirrus:** He seemed fond of playing the waiting game — I merely capitalized on that. While you were all out on a wild goose chase, I rallied other heliobi to my cause, thereby increasing my strength!

> **浮烟：**你们的将军喜欢「缓兵之计」，我就顺势而为，为各位出谋划策，令岁阳们在此汇聚，成为我的助力！

**Cirrus:** Thanks to all of you, I've amassed the power of every heliobus sealed within Fyxestroll Garden. All I need to do now is wait for the general to make his grand entrance.

> **浮烟：**多亏了你们，我已汇聚封印在绥园中所有岁阳的力量。万事俱备，只需要静候将军入局。

**Cirrus:** Before that happens, I should keep my end of the bargain: destroying you without leaving a trace.

> **浮烟：**在此之前，我应当先遵照约定，将你们这些车前小卒，杀个片甲不留！

*(Begin battle against Cirrus (Boss) ×20)*

### Defeat Cirrus

> *Cirrus, having distracted the attention of everyone, gathered its power once again while the judges were absent from Fyxestroll Garden. Evidently, its plans failed once more. Cirrus perceives this part of its plan as a step to force Jing Yuan into a fight.*

**Huohuo:** They keep on coming back for more... What should we do, Tail?

> **藿藿：**这家伙无论打倒多少次，总是能复原……尾巴，我们该怎么办？

**Tail:** Take it easy, kid. Heliobi might lack physical form, but they're not impervious to physical attacks. Cirrus is merely putting on a brave face.

> **尾巴：**放心吧，虽然岁阳没有形体，但并非无法被有形之物击败，浮烟终究只是在硬撑而已。

**Tail:** The moment they sense even a hint of defeat, it's over — they'll break into pieces, regardless of how powerful they are.

> **尾巴：**一旦它在精神层面认为自己已经输掉了，即便是再强大的岁阳也再难像这样聚合起来。

**Huohuo:** So just like what happened to Ignamar, once a heliobus realizes they're going to lose, they'll shatter?

> **藿藿：**就像是「燎原大战将军」一样，岁阳意识到「败局注定」时，就会溃不成形吗？

**Tail:** Exactly. And If there's anyone who can cause Cirrus to shatter, it's him...

> **尾巴：**没错。要说现在谁能令它真正溃败的话，也只有那个人了……

**Cirrus:** So, general, you've finally decided to show up. Ready to face the music?

> **浮烟：**你终于来了，罗浮的将军。终于愿意与我对局了？

**Jing Yuan:** Defeat my army, and I'll give you the rematch you seek. My word holds true.

> **景元：**「杀穿中阵，我便与你重续当年的战斗。」我一诺千金。

**Jing Yuan:** As I mentioned previously, when Teng Xiao and Ignamar fought, the scales were balanced.

> **景元：**不过，就像我之前所说，腾骁与燎原，彼时交战的双方可谓旗鼓相当。

**Jing Yuan:** But for you, Cirrus, this game was never about fairness, was it? And yet here we are — my power decidedly outmatches yours.

> **景元：**但对于你，浮烟，这场枰上棋争打从一开始就没有公平的原则——我的实力完全在你之上。

**Jing Yuan:** Notions of rules and equal competition are mere illusions. Now, if you don't mind, allow me to show you how the endgame is played.

> **景元：**「遵守规则」、以及「势均力敌」都是弈手常有的幻觉，就让我来教你如何终盘吧。

*(Begin battle against Cirrus (Boss) ×20)*

**Jing Yuan:** Accept your defeat, you are bested.

> **景元：**投子认负吧，你已经输了。

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Cirrus:** The fate of humans means nothing to me.

> **浮烟：**人类的胜负于我毫无意义。

**Cirrus:** We play by our own set of rules.

> **浮烟：**我们有自己的游戏规则。

**Jing Yuan:** Checkmate.

> **景元：**将军

*(Cutscene ends)*

**Cirrus:** ...I concede.

> **浮烟：**…我认输了。

**Jing Yuan:** Good.

> **景元：**承让。

**Cirrus:** I am no Ignamar... I'm nothing... nothing more than a faint flicker.

> **浮烟：**我终究不是燎原，我只是…一团黯淡的火焰，无用之物。

**Jing Yuan:** I know of no "faint flicker" that could strategize such a fierce battle against one of the Reignbow Arbiter's generals.

> **景元：**敢于挑战帝弓的将军，并且凭计谋令将军入局鏖战。这可不是「无用之物」能做到的。

**Cirrus:** Listening to a winner feign humility is enough to make one sick.

> **浮烟：**身为胜者又故作谦逊的发言，真是教人作呕。

**Jing Yuan:** The heliobi may reflect our desires, but their fervent blaze... is undeniably awe-inspiring. Neither General Teng Xiao nor myself would deride such a foe.

> **景元：**岁阳一族所映照的虽然是人类自身的欲望，但这份锐烈燃烧的样子……确实壮丽。无论是腾骁将军还是我，都不会嘲笑这样的对手。

**Cirrus:** I have been bested, it's true... I only sought to lock horns with you, regardless of victory or defeat. Next...

> **浮烟：**输了就是输了——浮烟只求与将军一战，不求胜负，已经满足，接下来……

**Jing Yuan:** Next is eternal punishment. For an inextinguishable flame such as yourself, I see no punishment more fitting than to see you confined to a vacant abyss, forever lamenting your past transgressions.

> **景元：**接下来，等待你的将是永罚。对长生不灭的火焰而言，失去自由，困于空无一物的虚空囚牢中，比终结其存续更可怕。

**Jing Yuan:** Such is the fate that awaits you, Cirrus.

> **景元：**浮烟，等待着你的，就是这样的命运。

**Cirrus:** Hehe, so be it...

> **浮烟：**是啊，但那又如何……

**Cirrus:** Mountains will crumble, oceans will evaporate, but the heliobi will always be there, patiently waiting for their day of freedom... and that day will come.

> **浮烟：**群山会塌陷，大海会干涸，岁阳会等着，等着重获自由的那一天再度到来。那一天也终将到来。

**Cirrus:** As long as the generals of the Xianzhou live, my battle against them will continue, until the stars themselves burn out.

> **浮烟：**只要仙舟的将军还存在，我将继续与仙舟的将军战斗下去，直到星辰熄灭。

### Talk to Hanya

> *With General Jing Yuan's assistance, you have protected Luofu from the hidden peril that few are aware of. The ghost-hunting squad contributed quite a lot to it. Ask Madam Hanya about her thoughts on this matter.*

### (Optional) Talk to Jing Yuan

*(Talk to Jing Yuan)*

**Jing Yuan:** Cirrus's will to fight has been shattered — they can no longer pose a threat. Considering that everything here is now under control, I'll take my leave.

> **景元：**浮烟战意瓦解，已不再能成为祸患。看来这里事情已经解决，我也该回去了。

- ▸ Thanks for stepping in, General.

**Jing Yuan:** I merely fulfilled my duty, and kept my promise to Cirrus.

> **景元：**应尽之责而已，也兑现了对浮烟的承诺。

- ▸ My masterstroke wasn't that bad after all?

**Jing Yuan:** Not at all. You have been of great assistance these past days, and for that I express my gratitude.

> **景元：**没错没错，这几日全仰赖开拓者了，景元在此谢过。

**Jing Yuan:** But the heliobus we dealt with will not be easily dismissed. Given sufficient time, and regardless of how slim the odds are, they will someday break the seal that binds them. However, that concern will fall to the generals of the future.

> **景元：**不知道这燎原的碎片以后还会与多少位罗浮将军交手，如此想来，作为一介武人还有些羡慕。
